guruñca jñānadātāraṃ kṛṣṇānandena dhīmatā

guruśiṣyayoḥ

gururityabhidhīyate

brāhmaṇottamaḥ | tapasvī satyavādī ca gṛhastho gururucyate

sa yāti nakaraṃ ghoraṃ sā pūjā viphalā bhavet

śāpamavāpnuyāt

vāmanaḥ | kunakhī śyāvadantaśca strījitaścādhikāṅgakaḥ

(vihīnaḥ pāṭhāntara) yaḥ sa guruḥ śiṣyasammataḥ

kriyāhīnaṃ śaṭhañcāpi vāmanaṃ gurunindakam

guruṃ tantreṇa varjayet

vāmanastathā | ityādyapi bodhyamiti

dānadhyānaparāyaṇ

dīnāyācāraśūnyāya yantradveṣaparāya ca

gurubhaktivihīnāya na deyā malināya ca

kṛpaṇāstatathā | daridrā rogiṇo ruṣṭā rāgiṇo bhogalālasāḥ

anyāyopārjitadhanāḥ paradāraratāśca ye

bhraṣṭācārāśca ye kaṣṭavṛttayaḥ piśunāḥ khalāḥ

parityājyāḥ śiṣyatvenopakalpitāḥ

gurutā śiṣyatā vāpi tayorvatsaravāsataḥ

| svapne tū niyamo na hīti nāradavacanāt

tathā śiṣyārjjitaṃ pāpaṃ guruḥ prāpnoti niścitam

śiṣyayogyatā

na hi devāḥ prasīdanti kalau cānya vidhānataḥ

teṣāmāgamamārgeṇa siddhirna śrautavartmanā

teṣā bhidā na karttavyā yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ

dīkṣāvyākhyāṃ prabhutvañca guroragre parityajet

na kuryādguruṇā sārddhaṃ śiṣyo bhūtvā kadācana

ukārācchivatādātmyaṃ dadyāditi guruḥ smṛtaḥ

andhakāranirodhitvādgururityabhidhīyate

kulārṇave sarvaśātrārthavettā ca gṛhastho gururucyate

daive pitre'rimitre ca gṛhastho deśiko bhavet

tasya tu | na kadācidbhavet siddhirna mantrairdaivapūjanaiḥ

krośamātra sthito bhūtvā guruṃ pratidinaṃ namet

ekadhā uttarāyaṇe karttavyam

sodarasya kaniṣṭhasya vairipakṣāśritasya vā

vanavāsinaḥ | viviktāśramiṇo dīkṣā na sā kalyāṇadāyikā

sutām | na putrañca tathā bhrātā bhrātaraṃ na ca dīkṣayet

dūṣyatīti vacanāt

syādbhautike'pi ca

labhet priye | tadaiva tāntu dīkṣeta tyaktvā guruvicāraṇam

prāyaścattantu ayutasāvitrījapaḥ sarvatra darśanāt

śaṅkha : daśasāhasryajāpena sarvakalmaṣanāśinī

jyeṣṭhaputrāya dadyādityādi

mahātrīrthaṃ uparāge sati sarvatra na doṣaḥ

vaiśampāyanasaṃhitāyāṃ śaunakaṃ prati vyasavacanām

| svapnalabdhaṃ striyādattaṃ saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati

mātuścāṣṭaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || idastu gurorupāsitamantraparam

yoginītantre strīpadaṃ vidhavāparaṃ ekavākyatābalāt

nārakī bhave

tatkalase prakṣipya uttolya mantraṃ gṛhṇīyādityarthaḥ

nāradavacanāt | tatra siddhādiniyamo nāsti

kalambāśca kāmbojāścādhamā matāḥ

tasmāddīkṣati sā proktā munibhistantravedibhiḥ

sadgatiḥ | tasmāt sarvaprayatnena guruṇā dīkṣito bhavet

kurvīttāntrikāt

gṛhṇāti narādhamaḥ | manvantarasahasreṣu niṣkṛtinaiva jāyate

tīrthagamanenā'pi na ca śarīrayantraṇaiḥ

92 || tatkṛtaṃ tasya vā śrāddhaṃ sarvaṃ yāti hyadhogagim

nirayamāpnoti brāhmaṇo yātyadhogatim

jānīyāt sa mṛto'dhogacchati

dīkṣādhikārī syādanyathā pāpabhāgbhavet

yasmāttasmānmantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ

śodhayet

sarveṣu siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet

mahāśabdena nīyate

vādhitāḥ

sāmpradāyikāḥ

āgneyañcāmbahsāṃ ripuḥ

rāmārcanacandrikādhṛtatvācca

ripurmantrādyavarṇa syāttena tasyāhitaṃ bhavet

yatnataḥ iti vacanāt | tantrarājaghṛtatvācca

sañcitavyayaḥ

bheditān varṇān revatyaṃśagatān kramāt

rohiṇī | imāni mānuṣānyāhurnakṣatrāṇi manīṣiṇaḥ

devatārakāḥ

saptamāni nakṣatrāṇi varjanīyāni

bheditāḥ

catuścatukoṣṭhe ṣoḍaśa koṣṭha iti yāvat

sampadyante tathā likhet

puṣṇāti poṣako'bhīṣṭaṃ ghātako nā śayed - dhruvam

dviguṇādhikāt | tatsiddho grahādeva susiddhāriḥ svagotrahā

patnīghnastadarirhanti sādhakam

tat | santyajya tat devatāyāstasyā anyaṃ bhajenmanum

mānavedibhiḥ

bhagavāna śivaḥ

likhettataḥ | ṛ ṝ varṇadvayaṃ ḷ ḹ taddhi klīvaṃ pracakṣate

gopālaviṣayakameva | gopāle'ka'kaḍamaḥ smṛta iti vacanāt

śaivañcākaḍamaṃ smṛtam | iti yāmalīyāt

gopāle'kaḍamaḥ smṛtaḥ

dhanaṃ syāt

mantraḥ śbhado bhavati dhanī cenna

ītyuktvā ṣaṭkāla - kāla ityuktam

tulyaṃ yadā samaphalaḥ kathito mūnīndraiḥ

tu prāptimātreṇa sarvasaddhistu jāyate

tāvatsaṃkhyāṃ saptaguṇaṃ kṛtvā tribhirharet

adhikaṃ ṛṇaṃ śeṣaṃ dhanaṃ jñeyaṃ

yojayet kramāt

gurṇā yat kṛtaṃ bhavet

vadatyanyamanasko'pi tannāma grāhyameva ca

japedbudhaḥ

smṛtam | kālikāyāśca tārāyāstārācakraṃ śubhāvaham

munisattamaiḥ

ṛṇe ca prāptimātreṇa sarvāsiddhistu jāyate

darbhaśayyāṃ pariṣkṛtya śiṣyaṃ tatra niveśayet

śiṣyābhimantraṇam

yoginītantrāt nānyatra

duḥkhaprado nityamevaṃ māsavidhikramaḥ

dvitīyāyāṃ bhavejjñānaṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ śucirbhavet

ṣaṣṭhyāṃ jñānakṣayaḥ saukhyaṃ labhate saptamīdine

aṣṭamyāṃ buddhināśaṃ syānnavamyāṃ vapuṣaḥ kṣayaḥ

daśamyāṃ rājasaubhāgyekādaśyāṃ śucirbhavet

dvādaśyāṃ sarvaśuddhiḥ syāt trayodaśyā daridratā

dharmavṛddhiḥ syādasvādhyāyaṃ vivarjayet

sandhyāgarjitanirghoṣabhūkampollakānipātanam

śaivāgamāntare

kṛttikāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī rohiṇyāṃ vākpatirbhavet

pūrvaphalgunyāṃ prānoti ca na saṃśayaḥ

sutrahāniḥ syānmūlāyāṃ kīrtivarddhanam

saukhyañcottarabhādre ca revatyāṃ kīrtivarddhanam

praśasyate | yadīśasya kṛśānorvā mantrārambho yathākramam

tatrāmaviṣayamagastyasaṃhitoktatvāt

tathā yogaḥ sarvatrantre śubhāvahāḥ

harṣaṇaḥ | varīyāṃśca śivaḥ siddho brahma aindraśca ṣoḍaśa

śubhānyeva sarvatantreṣu bhāṣitam

candratārānukūle ca kuryāddīkṣāpravarttanam

dvisvabhāvataṃ lagnaṃ śaktimantre praśasyate

sarvanāśakāḥ

śuklapakṣe śubhā dīkṣā kṛṣṇe'pyāpañcamaddināt

śobhanā

śaṅkareṇa ca bhāṣitam

| jyeṣṭhe caturdaśī kṛṣṇā āṣāḍhe nāgapañcamī

kālavinirṇayaḥ

śaktiviṣayam || 23 || caturthīti gaṇeśaviṣayaṃ tattatkalpoktatvāt

sūryaparvaṇi | evaṃ candragrahaṇe'pi

bhavet | iti yoginīhṛdayācca

athavāpyanurādhāyāṃ revatyāṃ vā praśasyate

viśeṣeṇa kārtike ca viśeṣataḥ

caturdaśī | saptamī ravivāre ca sūryaparvaśartaḥ samāḥ

tāsu dīkṣāṃ samācaret

śrīgirau kāśyāṃ kālākāṃ na śodhayet

tithiḥ | kṛtā tāsu budhairdīkṣā sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradā

śodhayet

tridinaspṛśi | tatra lagnādikaṃ kiñcinna vicārya kathañcana

viṣuvāyanayordvandve naiva kiñcidvicārayet

śubhāvahāḥ

guhāsu ca | gaṅgāyāstu taṭe vāpi koṭikoṭiguṇaṃ bhavet

gṛhṇīyāttato dīkṣā tīrthaṣveteṣu pārvati

daśaparvasu saṃjapet

aṣṭottaraśataviṣayam viṣṇuviṣayañca

samākhyātā sarvatantrapradīpikā

idamapyaṣṭottaraśatādiviṣayam

| idamapyaṣṭottaraśataviṣayam

viyogācca chidre ca sravate japaḥ

parvasandhiṣu yajjaptaṃ tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet

rākṣasāstena gaṇayet sarvathā budhaḥ

sudhīḥ | asaṃkhyākārakasyāsya sarvaṃ bhavati niṣphalam

ācchādya vāsasā hastau dakṣiṇena japet sadā

yādṛśī mālā saṃkhyāne'pi ca tādṛśī

ṭu tu pu yu śavoṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ

ityupalakṣaṇam

caramārṇaṃ merurūpaṃ laṅghanaṃ naiva kārayet

pratirohataḥśatamayīṃ vargāṣṭakāṣṭottaram

lakāradvayaṃ jñeyamiti

kuśamūlaiśca gṛhasthasyākṣamālikā

mokṣañca na dadāti priyaṃkarī

prayatnena jananyā jāravat priye

prayacchedvipulaṃ dhanam

jape śastā rudrākṣaṃ raktacandanaiḥ

karṇanetrāntarasthāsthi mahāśaṅkhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ

caturottaraiḥ | aṣṭottaraśataiḥ sarvasiddhiruktā manīṣibhaḥ

celājinakuśottaramiti bhagavadvacanācca

paṭājinakuśottara iti

viśedyatirvanasthaśca brahmacārī ca bhikṣukaḥ

ṣaṭkarmasu pratyekaṃ vihitāsanāni

tanniṣphalaṃ vidyāt kruddhā bhavati devatā

| gopucchasadṛśī mālā yadvā sarpākṛtiḥ śubhā

prakalpayet | ekaikaṃ mātṛkāvarṇaṃ grathanādau tu saṃjapet

ityetābhyāmicchā vikalpaḥ

nirmāya mālāṃ vai śodhayenmunisattama

sadyojātena sajjalaiḥ

dvijasattamaḥ

kṛtvā raktapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet | iti śaktiviṣayam

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā sampātājyaṃ vinikṣipet

bhavenmṛtyustasmādyatnaparo bhavet

gopayedyatnato gṛhī

aṃguṣṭhamadhyamāyogāt sarvasiddhiḥ suniścitā

jyeṣṭhākaniṣṭhāyogena śatruṇāṃ nāśanaṃ matam

japenniṣiddhasaṃsparśaṃ kṣālayitvāyathoditam

śataṃ japediti | karabhraṣṭa - chinnayostulyaphalakatvāt

sampūjya ca guruṃ gṛhṇīyānmālikāṃ śubhām

naraḥ | tataḥ puraskriyāṃ kuryānmantrasiddhikāmyayā

tīrthapradeśāḥ sindhunāṃ saṅgamaḥ pāvanaṃ vanam

nijālayam | sādhane ca praśastāni sthānānyetāni mantriṇām

cittaṃ prasīdati

koṭirdevālaye puṇyamanantaṃ śivasannidhau

japenmantrī tatra kūrmaṃ vicintayet

vāstau gṛhe tañca vicintayet

prayatnena śastānnāśī bhavennaraḥ

guḍavarjitam | tilāścaiva sitā mudgāḥ kandaḥ kemukavarjitaḥ

prajapedyāvattāvanniṣphalamucyate

svātvotpādanāt

tataḥ āvaraṇapūjā

dhūpayedājyasammiśrairnīcairdevasya deśikaḥ

dahet | tataḥ samarpayeddhūpaṃ ghaṇṭāvādyajayasvanaiḥ

tantre : na bhūmau vitareddhūpaṃ nāsane na ghaṭe tathā

darśanāt

pakvañca devatāvāme āmānnañcaiva dakṣiṇe

suropamam | iti sāmpradāyikāḥ

dakṣakarṇe iti dvijātiviṣayam

eṣa vidhidvijātīnāṃ strīśūdrāṇāñca vāmataḥ

kīrtiśrīkāntiputrāyurbalārogyaṃ sadā'stu te | iti utthāpayet

tathā tasminkṣaṇe devi japenmantraṃ śatāṣṭakam

nocetsañcāriṇīśaktirgurumeti na saṃśayaḥ

tapanañcaiva pūjākrama udāhṛtaḥ

āgneyāṃ gaṇanāthañca nai-ṛtyāṃ tapanantathā

svasthānavarjitā devā duḥkhaśokabhayapradāḥ

hānidāḥ

vaikalpikamiti sāmpradāyikāḥ

āhvānaṃ nijamudrikaviracanaṃ dhyānaṃ prabhoḥ pūjanam

pratiṣṭhitaprati mādiyantrādiviṣayam

devatāśāpamāpnuyāt

tathā ca : siddhādiśodhanaṃ naiṣāmaṅgatve sati rājavat

dīkṣāṃ vinaiva karttavya upadeśaḥ sadaiva hi

panasāmrāśvatthavaṭavakulāni

vakulameva ca | pañcapallavamityuktaṃ munibhistantravedibhiḥ

padmarāgaṃ marakataṃ nīlañceti yathākramāt

mantritaḥ prokṣaṇītoyaiḥ śāntikumbhajalaistathā

aṣṭottaraśataḥ

japetkarṇe upadeśe tvayaṃ vidhiḥ | iti saṃkṣepadīkṣā

yuge yuge ca karttavya upadeśaḥ kalau yuge

bilvādipatrajaṃ śyāmamityuktaṃ varṇapañcakam

kramāt | maṇḍalaṃ sarvatobhadrametsādhāraṇaṃ matam

proktaṃ sarvasamṛddhidam

dakṣiṇāṃ datvā mahāntamutsavaṃ kuryāt

tantrasāre prathamaḥ paricchedaḥ

vidyā kuṇḍalinīrūpā maṇḍalatrayabhedinī

tatprabhāpaṭalavyāptaṃ śarīramai cintayet

yena vinā na siddhḥ syānnarakañca prapadyate

niṣphalā tasya pūjā syācchaucahīnā yathā kriyā

iti vacanāttasyāvaśyakatvaṃ

vaidikī tāntrikī sandhyā yathānukramayogataḥ

purato vajrapāṣāṇe nikṣipedastramuccaran

viśeṣastu snānaprakaraṇe vaktavyaḥ

gāyatrī tu snāna prakarane vaktavyā

tāvanna pūjayedviṣṇuṃ śaṅkaraṃ vā maheśvarīm

sandhyāyāṃ patitāyāntu gāyatrīṃ daśadhā japet

nadyādau datvā vaidikasnānaṃ kṛtvā tāntrikasnānamācaret

mantrasnānaṃ tataḥ kuryātkarmaṇā siddhihetave

kuryāt

gṛhītvāmukadevasya prītaye snānāmācaret

karma kuryāt

dhyātvā jalāñjalīn kṣiptā tarpayediṣṭadevatām

gurupaṃki purā tarpya tarpayediṣṭadevatām

oṃ nāradaṃ tarpayāmi ityādikrameṇa prayogaḥ

tarpayāmipadañcoktvā namontaṃ tarpayettataḥ

dvitīyānteṣu cetyevaṃ tarpaṇasya manuḥ smṛtaḥ

homatarpaṇayoḥ svāheti tattanmantravacanāt

svāhāntaṃ tarpayantvevam ityādi

kulāmṛtavacanāt

tatrāśaktaścenmūlamantramuccārya iṣṭadevatāmātraṃ tarpayet

aśaktau mūlamuccārya devīmātraṃ pratarpayet

svāhāntaṃ mantramuccāryaṃ arghyaṃ datvā japenmanum

idamarghyaṃ dattvā, tattaddevatā gāyatrīṃ śatadhā vā japet

iti daśadhā śaktāśaktabhedena

iti yāmalavacanāt

iti garuḍagāyatrī

gadāpadmadharāṃ devīṃ sūryāsana - kṛtāśrayām

sūryamaṇlamadhyasthāṃ dhyāyan devīṃ samabhyaset

svecchāgṛhītavapuṣīṃ guru vidyākṣarātmikām

paritaḥ svātmamukhyābhiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśattattvaśaktibhiḥ

cintayitvā bhagavatīṃ nityābhiḥ parivāritām

anyatra kālikāmantre ekajaṭāpadasthāne kālikāpadaprayogaḥ

namaskṛtya, yāgasthānamāviśediti snānavidhiḥ

kuryāt

vāyuṃ recayet

so'hamevaṃ samābhāsya jīvaṃ hṛdi samānayet

smaraṇānnarakaṃ yāti dīkṣā ca viphalā bhavet

jīvaṃ tejomayaṃ dhyātvā namomantreṇa yojayet

avyayabrahmasaṃyogādbhūtaśuddhiriyaṃ mateti

brahmamārgaḥ suṣumnā

susūkṣme

tenaiva vidhinā devi sthiro kuryānnijāṃ tanum

ṣuṣumnāvartmanātmānaṃ paramātmani yojayet

ityantarmārtṛkāṃ nyasya sarvāṅganyāsamācaret

etāśca mātṛkāmudrāḥ rameṇa parikīrtitāḥ

śrīkaṇṭhādyānimānnyasyetsarvamantraḥ prasīdati

śrīvidyāviṣaye mantrī vāgbhavādyaṣṭasiddhaye

viparītaphalaṃ dadyādabhaktyā pūjanaṃ yathā

aṃguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ vā cānyathā viphalaṃ bhavet

śyāmādividyāyāṃ viśeṣamātṛkānyāso vaktavyaḥ

prāṇāyāmaḥ sa vijñeyastarjanīmadhyame vinā

sagarbho mantrajāpena nirgarbho mātrayā bhavet

kālena mātrā sājñeyā munibhirvedapāragai'

yaugike punarmātrāniyamaḥ

tattatkalpokta pīṭhaśaktīrmadhye pīṭhamanuñca nyaset

saṃsādhayati śuddhātmā sa tasya ṛṣirīritaḥ

gurutvānmastake cāsya nyāsastu parikīrtitaḥ

akṣaratvātpadatvācca mukhe chandaḥ samīritam

hṛdayāmbhojamadhyasthā devatā tatra tāṃ nyaset

daurbalyaṃ yāti mantrāṇāṃ viniyogamajānatām

śaktiñca pādayoścaiva sarvāṅge kīlakaṃ nyaset

devatābhāvamāpnoti mantrasiddhiḥ prajāyate

dṛṣṭvāvighnāḥ palāyante siṃhaṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā gajāḥ

sarvavighnaiḥ sa bādhyaḥ syādvyāghrairmṛgaśiśuryathā

aṃgulīnāmiti vacanāditi sarvatra sādhāraṇam

tisṛbhistarjanīmadhyamānāmābhiḥ

iti rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtavacanāt

ṣaḍaṅgamantrānityuktān ṣaḍaṅgeṣu niyojayet

iti śāradāvacanam

viśvagviṣaktaḥ kathitāstramudrā yatrākṣiṇī tarjanīmadhyame ca

svanāmādyakṣaraṃ bījaṃ sarveṣāmabhidhīyate

kuryādvahirarcanam

śaṅkhasthāpantu dīkṣāprakaraṇe uktam

iti darśanātśarīre pīṭhapūjā

oṃ parāparagurubhyo namaḥ, oṃ parameṣṭhigurubhyo namaḥ

tripurādau tu viśeṣagurupūjā vaktavyā

madhye madhye ca śaktīrnava samabhiyajetpīṭhamantreṇa bhūyaḥ

tārādividyādau tu viśeṣo vaktavyaḥ

savyañca dakṣiṇaṃ vidyāddakṣiṇañcottaraṃ matam

anena vidhinā mantrī pūrvādau pūjanaṃ caret

ṣaṭkoṇakarṇikaṃ tatra vedadvāropaśobhitam

prāṇapratiṣṭhāntaṃ karma kuryat

saṃyojya - yantramadhye tatsaṃsthāpyāvāhayettataḥ

tataḥ ṣoḍaśopa"cāraiḥ pañcopacārairvā pūjayet

prayojayedarcanāyāmupacārastu ṣoḍaśaḥ

gandhādayo naivedyāntā pūjā pañcoparacārikā iti

prayojayedarcanāyāmupacārāndaśa kramāt

ityādinā syāt

tato dhūpadīpau dadyāt

dṛṣṭiparyantaṃ dīpaṃ dadyāt

iti vacanādupārānarghyapātrasthalenotsṛjya dadyāt

vaiṣṇave tu naivedye viśeṣo vaktravyaḥ

dadyāt

mantrāte karmasannipāta iti nyāyāt

namaskārānantaraṃ vā

iti japaṃ samarpyaṃ stutvā natvā'ṣṭāṅgapramāṇaṃ kuryāt

sahasrajanmajaṃ pāpaṃ tyaktvā vaikuṇṭhamāpnuyāt

śaṅkhahastena sarvatra pradakṣiṇaṃ prakīrtitam

tataśca dakṣiṇaṃ gatvā namaskārastrikoṇavat

somasūtraṃ jalaniḥsaraṇasthānam

tridhā ca veṣṭayetsamyagdevatāyāḥ pradakṣiṇe

akāle darśanaṃ viṣṇorhanti puṇyaṃ purākṛtam

suṣumnāvartmanā puṣpamāghrāyodvāsayettataḥ

viśvaksenaḥ smṛto viṣṇostejaścaṇḍo vivasvata ityādi

anyadyathāyogyāya dattvā yathāsukhaṃ viharediti

viṣṇoriti tattadevatāparam

nirmālyañca śucau deśe naivedyaṃ bhakṣayetsudhīḥ

aṅgalagnaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati

bhuvaneśvarīṃ tām

brahmādayo'pi vāṃ jñātvā lebhire paramāṃ śriyam

arddhacandro'nusvāraḥ

ata eva maheśāni bhuvaneśīti kathyate

karṇikāyāṃ hrīṃ sarvaśaktikamalāsanāya namaḥ

hatūryabījaśaktī ca kīlakaṃ repha ucyate

satyādi pañcahrasvāḍhyā nyastavyā bhūtasaṃprabhāḥ

sarvatra karāṅganyāse evaṃ kramaḥ

nyastavyaṃ vadane mūlaṃ bhūyaścaitāṃ stanau nyaset

savyāṃse parśvayugale nābhideśe ca deśikaḥ

evaṃ sarvatra

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya vahiḥpūjāmārabheta

caturasraṃ caturdvārameva maṇḍalamālikhet

sarvatra praṇavādinamo'ntena pūjayet

savāhanāyeti kramadīpikā

asya puraścaraṇaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣajapaḥ

trisvāduryuktairjuhuyādaṣṭadravyairdaśāṃśataḥ

trisvādviti ghṛtamadhuśarkarā iti

ṣaḍaṅgāni manorasya jātiyuktena deśikaḥ

saumyāṃ ratnaghaṭasthasavyacaraṇāṃ dhyāyetparāmambikām

taddaśāṃśaṃ juhuyānmantrī pīṭhe prāgīrite yajeditivacanāt

pūrvavannyāsaḥ

ṣaḍaṅgānītyādi

evaṃ dhyātvā pūrvavadyajet

anyat sarvaṃ pūrvavadbodhām

caturviṃśatilakṣamiti kecit

tām

ṣoḍaśadale pūjāyā anuktatvātṣoḍaśadalābhāvaḥ

annapūrṇa ṭhayugalaṃ manuḥ saptadaśākṣaraḥ

annapradāmokṣadā ca sadā vibhavadāyinī

śrībījādyā yadā devi tadā sukhavivarddhinī

vibhavadāyinī śrīmāyāyugmabījādyā sarvasampattipūraṇī

paṃktiśchandaḥ samākhyātaṃ devatā cānnapūrṇikā

kalpe ca: yadvījādyā bhavedvidyā tadbījenāṅgakalpanā

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

śūdrasya praṇavaṃ devi kathitaṃ tantravedibhiḥ

yadyadbījādiko mantrastenaivāṅga prakalpanā

śrīmāyāmadanaiḥ prokto mantro bījatryātmakaḥ

madhye devīṃ samāvāhya dhyāyetsarvaṃ samṛddhidām

dviruktaistadbījairaṅgāni ṣaṭkramāt

ekārastadyuktaḥ kṣaḥ, punarbhuvaneśvarī phaṭ

tvaritā devatā proktā puruṣārthaphalapradā

mūlena vyāpakaṃ kuryāt

vinyasya vyāpakaṃ kuryātsamastenaiva sādhakaḥ

dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ ṣaḍaṅgāni kuryātsādhakasattamaḥ

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

drave vahnivadhūrmantro dvādaśārṇo'yamīritaḥ

vācā kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅgāni nityāṃ dhyāyedyathāvidhi

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ saṃpūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

iti bhaṭṭaḥ

hrāmādyaiḥ saha kurvīta ṣaḍaṅgāni yathāvidhi

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

durgāyai hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādinā pūjayet

tato dhūpādi visarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

vācaniko'ṣṭasahasrahomaḥ

vadhūvījādiḥ kavacādiśca navākṣaraḥ

kāmaṃ praṇavamityuktaṃ bhavedvidyā punardaśa

tataḥ pūrvokta - ṛṣyādinyāsañca kṛtvā karāṅganyāsau kuryāt

tarjaniamapi vibhratīṃ nijabāhubhiḥ śaśiśekharām

iti vacanāt śaṅkhaniṣedhaḥ

dīrghādyatvametāsāntu

tasastrāṇi siddhamayī prayogañca samācaret

ityādi vacanāt

balidānasya mantro'yaṃ mardinyāḥ parikīrtitaḥ

dviṭhānto jayadurgayaṃ vidyā vedyā daśākṣarī

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya arghyasthānaṃ kuryāt

aṣṭākṣarodite pīṭhe pūrvavatpūjayetsudhīḥ

bhūtendriyākṣaraiḥ prokto grahakṣudravināśakṛt

vada vada vāgvādini vahnivallabhā iti daśākṣaraḥ

bhuvaneśīsaṃpuṭo'yaṃ mahāsārasvatapradaḥ

namaḥ sarvatra

puṇḍarīkaiḥ payo'bhyaktaistilairvā madhurāplutaiḥ

pūrvoktapīṭhaśaktiḥ pīṭhamanuñca nyaset

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

vānte sinyai priyā vahnermanuḥ prokto daśākṣaraḥ

ṛṣirdakṣo virāṭachando devatāśrī samīritā

astraṃ kamalarūpāyai namo'ntāḥ praṇavādikāḥ

vidhāyāvaraṇapūjāṃ kuryāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

daśāṃśaṃ juhuyānmantrī madhurākteḥ saroruhaiḥ

mahālakṣmyāḥ samuddiṣṭastārādyaḥ sarvasiddhidaḥ

mahālakṣmyai devatāyai namaḥ

śeṣānnyasetsaptavarṇān hṛdaye saptadhātuṣu

jñānaiśvaryādayaḥ proktāḥ ṣaṭkramādaṅgadevatāḥ

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

ayutadvayamiti vācanikavyavasthā

yakāra ekārayuktaḥ,

astrametaiḥ padaiḥ kuryātribījapuṭitaiḥ pṛthak

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthānaṃ kuryāt

anurāgāya mahālakṣmībāṇāya namaḥ evaṃ krameṇa pūjayet

daśāṃśaṃ saṃskṛte vahnau prākproktenaiva vartmanā

aṣṭākṣaro manuḥ prokto bhānorabhimatapradaḥ

pīṭhamantro'yamākhyāto dineśasya jagatpateḥ

ādityo devatā prokto dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalapradaḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

phaṭ dviṭhāntāḥ pṛthagīritāḥ

guhye iṃ bhāskarāya, caraṇayoḥ aṃ sūryāya

mantravarṇān nyasedaṣṭau pratyekaṃ praṇavādinā

samāpayet

atrāpi vācanika evāṣṭasahasrahomaḥ

sargānvito bhṛgurbhānostryakṣaro'yaṃ samīritaḥ

ṛṣyādinyāsaṃ kuryāt

mūrddhādikaṇṭhaparyantaṃ kramādvījatrayaṃ nyaset

ṣaḍdīrghayuktamadhyena bījenāṅgādikalpanā

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

tilairvā madhurāsiktairjuhuyādvijitendriyaḥ

puṭitaṃ vimbabījena sarvakāmaphalapradam

ṭāntaṃ dahananetrendusahitaṃ tadudāhṛtam

pūrvavat-ṛṣyādinyāsaṃ kuryāt

vyāpakaṃ mūlabījena kurvīta tadantaram

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya arghyasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

dakṣāṃśaṃ kamalaiḥ phullairjuhuyānmadhurokṣitaiḥ

vāksiddhimatulāṃ lakṣmīṃ saubhāgyamapi sādhayet

ajapākhyo manuḥ prokto dvyakṣaraḥ surapādapaḥ

devatā jagatāmādiḥ saṃproktā girijāpatiḥ

hsāṃ ṣaḍdīrghayuktena kuryādaṅgakriyāṃ manoḥ

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

daśāṃśaṃ juhuyānmantrī tataḥ siddho bhavennaraḥ

yasya saṃsmaraṇāt santo bhāvābdhe pāramāśritāḥ

pāvano nāma mantro'yaṃ prokto vasvakṣaraḥ paraḥ

vaiṣṇavācamanaṃ kuryāt

sacaturthīnamo'ntaiśca nāmabhirvinyaset sudhīḥ

hṛdi arddhalakṣmīharaye devatāyai namaḥ

arddhalakṣmīhariḥ proktaḥ śrībījena ṣaḍaṅgakam

sānusvāraḥ

namaḥ sarvatra

vidyuddāmasamābhāḥ syuḥ paṅkajābhayavāhavaḥ

oṃ śrīṃ aṃ keśavāya kīrttyai namaḥ ityādi

smṛti dhṛtiṃ mahālakṣmīṃ prāpyānte haritāṃ vrajet

kaṃ namaḥ parāya pṛthivītattvātmane namaḥ pādayoḥ

evaṃ tattvāni vinyasya prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret

sarvātmasaṃyogayogapadmapīṭhātmane namaḥ

prahvī satyā tatheśānānugrahā navamī smṛtā

pīṭhātmane hṛdanto'yaṃ mantrastārādiko hareḥ

mantrasya devī gāyatrī devatā viṣṇuravyayaḥ

sahasrolkāyāstramuktamaṅgakḷptiriyaṃ matā

ṇāṃ namo dakṣapārśve, yaṃ namo vāmapārśve

yaṃ vinā naiva tatsamyagāsuraṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet

gadāyāṃ padme ca vinyaset

śaṅkhacakragadāmbhojapādaṣvavahito nyaset

dvādaśākṣaramantraṃ nyaset

tataḥ kirīṭamantreṇa vyāpakaṃ vinyasettataḥ

asya vijñānamātreṇa vāsudevaḥ prajāyate

evaṃ nyāsaṃ purā kṛtvā dhyāyennārāyaṇaṃ param

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

pañcapātraṃ hareḥ śuddhaṃ nānyattatra niyojayet

sauvarṇe rājate raupye ityādi

oṃ samānāya svāha iti sarvāḍaṃgulīraṃguṣṭhena spṛśet

taddaśāṃśaṃ sarasijairjuhuyānmadhurāplutaiḥ

ṣaḍakṣaro'yamādiṣṭo bhajatāṃ kāmado maṇiḥ

śrīrāmāya devatāyai namaḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu rāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

namaḥ ityādinā ṣaḍaṅgāni pūjayet

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

juhuyādarcite vahnau brāhmaṇānbhojayettataḥ

ṣaḍakṣaraḥ ṣaḍvidhaḥ syāccaturvargaphalapradaḥ

agastyaḥ śrīśivaḥ prokto munayo'tra kramādime

dhyānapūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavacca samācaret

daśākṣaro'yaṃ mantraḥ syādvasiṭaḥ syādṛṣirvirāṭa

ādyaṃ bījaṃ dviṭhaḥ śaktiḥ kāmenāṅgakriyā matā

nābhyūrujānupādeṣu daśārṇānvinyasenmanoḥ

anyatsarvaṃ pūrvavat

dvyakṣaro mantrarājo'yaṃ sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ

tryakṣaro mantrarājaḥ syātsarvābhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ

brahmā ṛṣiḥ syādgāyatrīcchando rāmaśca devatā

eteṣāṃ dhyānapūjādikaṃ prāguktaṣaḍakṣaravat

rāśinakṣatravicāre punarbījarahitena vicāraḥ

luptabījasvabhāvatvāddaśākṣara ihocyate || iti gautamīyāt

nānyathā siddhaye bhavet

yadvā mūlenaiva mantreṇa sarvatra prāṇāyāmaḥ

tattanmantravarṇasaṃkhyākai recakāditrayaṃ kuryāt

mantrādhiṣṭhātṛdevatāyai durgāyai namaḥ iti durgā namaskuryāt

punaḥ sṛṣṭisthitīti pañcadhā kuryāt

asurāntakacakrāya svāhā kaniṣṭhābhyāṃ phaṭ

iti ṣṛṣṭinyāsaḥ

karttavyaḥ

etāni namo'ntāni vinyaset

kirīṭādinyāsastu sarvatra viṣṇumantre

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

daśāyutam

sasitaghṛtasusiktairārabheddhomakarma

iti sanatkumāravacanāt

vidhāya dhyāyet

veṇuṃ dhamanaghṛto dorbhyāṃ dhyeyaḥ kṛṣṇo divākare

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

samāpayet

juhuyāt saṃskṛte vahnau mantrī sarvārthasiddhaye

kāmabījādirākhyāto manuraṣṭādaśākṣaraḥ

asya puraścaraṇaṃ daśākṣarapaṭaloktam

śaktiśrīpūrvakaścāṣṭādaśārṇo viṃśadakṣaraḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

tato daśatattvāni vinyasya punarmūlena vyāpaka kuryāt

guṇāgni vedakaraṇakaraṇākṣyakṣarairmanoḥ

vyāpakaṃ vidhāya yathāśakti mudrāṃ badhvā dhyāyet

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

daśākṣara - caturdaśākṣara - ekādaśākṣarāṇāmiti

sāmānyayantramuddiṣṭamaṣṭādaśākṣare śṛṇu

anyathā tāpinyādivirodhaḥ syāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

caturlakṣaṃ hunedājyaiścatvāriṃśatsahasrakam

dvāviṃśatyakṣaro mantro vāgīśatvapradāyakaḥ

sṛṣṭisthiti tatpūjanaṃ nāsti tadvarṇābhāvāt

juhuyātkarmaṇānena mantrasiddhirbhaveddhruvam

daśārṇo manuvaryaśca bhavecchakrākṣaro'paraḥ

śrīkṛṣṇāya devatāyai namaḥ

anya nyāsapūjājapahomādi sarvaṃ daśākṣaravatkāryam

trailokyamohano viṣṇuḥ kathitastava yatnataḥ

trailokyamohano viṣṇurdevatā samudīritā

dīrghaṣaṭkayujānena kāmabījena kalpayet

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

hunettāvacca tarpayet

asya nyāsapūjādikaṃ sarva pūrvavat

svāheti mantrarājo'yaṃ bhajatāṃ surapādapaḥ

hṛdi śrīkṛṣṇāya devatāyai namaḥ

ityādi

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

karma samāpayet

daśāṃśaṃ juhuyānmantrī kusumairbrahmavṛkṣajaiḥ

śrīśaktismarakṛṣṇāya govindāya śiro manuḥ

ratnābhiṣekavadṛṣyādinyāsaṃ kuryāt

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu śrīṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

tattadvarṇābhāvāt asya puraścaraṇaṃ japahomaśca tathā

śiro'ntaḥ ṣoḍaśārṇo'yaṃ rukmiṇī vallabhāhvayaḥ

hṛdi rukmiṇīvallabhāya devatāyai namaḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

śaṇasūnapītavasano nānāvibhūṣo hariḥ

ayutaṃ juhuyāt padmairaruṇairmadhurāplutaiḥ

manto vikṛtiravyarṇāvācakrādyāṅkanāvimo

viṃśatyarṇoktapūjāṃ kuryāt

padmotpalakare tābhyāṃ śliṣṭaṃ cakragarojjvalam

daśalakṣaṃ japedājyairhunettāvatsahasrakam

śrīpūrvau ṅe'ntāvuccārya huṃ phaṭ svāheti kīrtyate

bījaśaktī ca tatsame

śiro'nto bālavapuṣe klīṃ kṛṣṇāya smṛto vudhaiḥ

hṛdi śrīkṛṣṇāya devatāyai namaḥ

tataḥ pūrvavanmudrādikaṃ pradarśya dhyāyet

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

tarpayettāvadeteṣāṃ manūnāṃ hutasaṃkhyayā

varṇayugmaiḥ samastena proktaṃ syādaṅgapañcakam

juhuyādbrahmavṛkṣotthasamidbhipāyasena vā

digvidikṣvarcayedetān indravajrādikān vahiḥ

sa sarvasampatsampūrṇo nityaṃ śuddhavrajetpadam

saṃprokto mārayugmāntaḥ saṃsthakṛṣṇapadena tu

pūrvadṛṣyādinyāsakarāṅganyāsau ca kṛtvā dhyāyet

trimadhvaktairvilvaphalaiścatvāriṃśatsahasrakam

ayaṃ manurityarthaḥ

śārdūla - kāmāṃkuśalalitagaṇākapladīptaṃ mukundam

madhutrayayutairhutvā cārcayet pūrvavaddharim

pradhānaṃ vaiṣṇave tantre mantro'yaṃ surapādapaḥ

vāsudevāya devatāyai namaḥ

samastena bhavedastramaṅgakalpanamīritam

dhyāyet

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

karma samāpayet

tatsahasraṃ prajuhuyāttilairājyapariplutaiḥ

caturdaśākṣaraḥ prokto mantro'yaṃ surapādapaḥ

varṇalakṣaṃ mantravarṇasamasaṃkhyalakṣam

mahābalāya ṭhadvandvaṃ manuraṣṭādaśākṣaraḥ

candrāntakalpite pīṭhe prāguktena samarcayet

dvābhyāmastramiti proktaṃ prakāro'ṅgasya sūribhiḥ

vidhāya dhyāyet

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

pāyasānnaṃ prajuhuyāddadhyannambā yathāvidhi

bodhayadvitayānto'yaṃ mantrastārādirīritaḥ

hṛdi śrīhayagrīvāya devatāyai namaḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

tato dhūpādivisarajanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

daśāṃśaṃ vaiṣṇave vahnau juhuyānmantravittamaḥ

ekākṣaro manuḥ proktaścaturvargaphalapradaḥ

hsāṃ hsīṃ hsūṃ hsaiṃ hsauṃ hsaḥ iti ṣaḍaṅgakalpanā

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

ājyena daśāṃśa homaḥ

svabījādirayaṃ mantraścaturvargaphalapradaḥ

anyatsarvamekākṣaravat

svāhānto manurākhyāto bījaḥ praṇavasampuṭaḥ

svāhānto manurākhyāto haṃsena sampuṭokṛtaḥ

etayormantrayoḥ pūrvoktahayagrīvamantravatsarvaṃ jñātavyam

hṛllekhāpuṭitaścet syātsarvakāmaphalapradaḥ

nṛsiṃhāya devatāyai namaḥ

tāvadbhirnayanaṃ proktamantraṃ syātkaraṇākṣaraiḥ

aparāṅge ca kakudi nyasedvarṇānyathākramam

praṇavādinamo'ntena pūjayet

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karmasamāpayet

pāyasānnaḥ prajuhuyādvidhivatpūjite'nale

ṣaḍakṣaro narahareḥ kathitaḥ sarvakāmadaḥ

nṛsiṃhāya devatāyai namaḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

tatsahasraṃ samiddhe'gnau toṣayedvasunā gurum

ekākṣaro manuḥ proktaḥ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ

aukāraḥ śaktiḥ ṣaḍdīrghayuktabījenāṅgakalpanā

vasulakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ ityādivacanāt

'ṣṭākṣaro manuḥ prokto bhajatāṃ kāmadomaṇiḥ

dhyānārcanādikaṃ mantrarājavat

vasulakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ ityādivacanāt

māyā tāraḥ

śaktaye namaḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

pāyasairhavanaṃ kāryaṃ saṃskṛte havyavāhane

vahnijāyāvadhirmantraḥ syāttrayastriṃśadakṣaraḥ

mahādaṃṣṭrāya astraṃ syāt pañcāṅgāmiti kalpayet

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

iti viṣṇumantraparicchedaḥ

manasā smarāmi

yaḥ pūrvamṛṣayaḥ prāptāḥ śivasāyujyamañjasā

prāsādākhyo manuḥ prokto bhajatāṃ kāmado maṇiḥ

ete rudrāḥ samākhyātā dhṛtaśūlakapālakaḥ

raktotpalakapālābhyāmalaṃkṛtakarāmbujāḥ

vāmadevo muniśchandaḥ paṃktirdevaḥ sadāśivaḥ

kaṭyāṃ pārśvadvaye vāmadevasya trayodaśakalā nyaset

oṃ sudhāyai kalāyai namaḥ

vinyasya dhyāyet

sarvatraiva praśasto'bjaḥ śivasūryārcanaṃ vinā iti

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

prasūnaiḥ karavīrotthairjuhuyāttaddaśāṃśataḥ

manuḥ

umāpataye devatāyai namaḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

āragvadhaḥ śoṇāluḥ

pañcākṣarasamāyukto vasuvarṇo manurmataḥ

ṛṣyādinyāsa - karāṅganyāsānkuryāt

vṛttottuṅgastanāgrenihitakaratalaṃ vedaṭaṅkeṣṭahastam

tatsahasraṃ prajuhayātpāyasānnerghṛtaplutaiḥ

sthirā jakāraḥ karṇa ūkāraḥ bhṛguḥ sakāraḥ

mṛtyuñjayo mahādevo devatāsya prakīrtitā

bhṛguṇā dīrghayuktena kuryādaṅgakriyāṃ manoḥ

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

dhyānapūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavacca samācaret

prayaccha ṭhadvayānto'yaṃ dvāviṃśatyakṣaro manuḥ

hṛdi dakṣiṇāmūrttaye devatāyai namaḥ

mantravarṇairvibhaktarvā kuryādaṅgakriyāṃ manorati

dakṣiṇāmūrtimoḍe

juhuyāt saghṛtaiḥ padmairdaśāṃśaṃ saṃskṛte'nale

anilo yakāraḥ, au svarūpaṃ, ṣaṣṭha ūkāraḥ

hṛdi arddhanārīśvarāya devatāyai namaḥ

rephādi - vyañjanaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ kuryādaṅgāni phaṭ kramāt

karma samāpayet

ayutaṃ madhurāsiktairjuhuyāttilataṇḍulaiḥ

nīlakaṇṭhātmako mantro viṣadvayaharaḥ paraḥ

hṛdi nīlakaṇṭhāya devatāyai namaḥ

tataḥ samāhito bhūtvā nīlakaṇṭhaṃ vicintayediti

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

haviṣā juhuyātsamyaksaṃskṛte havyavāhane

praṇavo hṛnnīlakaṇṭhāyāṣṭākṣaro'paraḥ

tāro hṛnnīlakaṇṭhāya mantraścāṣṭākṣaraḥ paraḥ

ṛṣirgāyatrīcchandaḥ nīlakaṇṭho devatā

pañcākṣaro manuḥ proktastārādyo'yaṃ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ

mukhe paṃkticchandase namaḥ, hṛdi īśānāya devatāyai namaḥ

pañcamūrtīrnyaset

ityanena vyāpakanyāsaṃ kuryāt

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya, arghyasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

atra tattvaśabdena ṣaṭtriṃśattattvamucyate antaraṅgatvāt

phaḍantaścaṇḍamantro'yaṃ trivarṇātmā samīritaḥ

caṇḍeśvaro devatā ityādi

sarvajvālinisaṃyuktaiḥ phaḍantairaṅgamācaret

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjyārghyasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

kṣetrapālasya īritaḥ

ātmeti śaktiḥ ṣaḍdīrghayuktena bījenaivāṅganyāsaṃ

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

caruṇā ghṛtasiktena tataḥ kṣetreśamarcayet

tasya puraścaraṇamayutasaṃkhyako japaḥ

parivārebhyaḥ svasvanāmabhirbaliṃ dadyāt

eṣa balividhiḥ sarvagrahanivārako vijayaśrīkaraśca bhavati

kāntimedhābalārogyatejaḥpuṣṭiyaśaḥ śriyaḥ

ekaviṃśatyakṣarātmā śaktiruddho mahāmanuḥ

hṛdi vaṭukabhairavāya devatāyai namaḥ

pañcahrasvāḍhyamīśānādiṣu yojayediti

aṅgāni jātiyuktāni praṇavādyāni kalpayet

vaṭukamaniśaṃ śūladaṇḍau dadhānam

bandhūkāruṇavāsasaṃ bhayaharaṃ devaṃ sadā bhāvaye

kiṅkiṇīnūpurāḍhyam

āyurārogyajananamapavargaphalapradam

tāmasaṃ śatruśamanaṃ kṛtyābhūtagadāpaham

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjyārghyasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

tato vyomapaṅkajadaleṣu asitāṅgādīnbhairavānyajet

praṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt

sarvāṅge krīṃ kīlakāya namaḥ

oṃ krāṃ aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ ityādinā vā

parigṛhītabhairavīyavākyādubhayameva yuktam

mūlenāṣṭaśatena vyāpakaṃ kuryāt

kṛte'sminnyāsavarye tu sarvaṃ pāpaṃ praṇaśyati

ātmavidyāśivaistattvaistattvanyāsaṃ samācaret

etattrayaṃ kāmyam

dhyāyet

sṛkvadvayagaladraktadhārāvisphuritānanām

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya arghyasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

pīṭhapūjāmārabhet

madhye tu vaindavaṃ cakraṃ bījamāyāvibhūṣitamiti

svarṇe raupye'tha lauhe vā cakraṃ kāryaṃ vidhānataḥ

pīṭhasyottare gurupaṃktipūjā

mūlena pādyādibhiḥ pūjayet

dadyāt

mahākālaṃ pūjayet

jaṭābhāralasaccandrakhaṇḍamugraṃ jvalannibham

pañcāpacoraiḥ pūjayet

phaṭsvāhayā samāyukto mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ

brahmayonisamutpanne gaccha devi mamāntaramiti mantreṇa

pītvā, nirmālyaṃ śirasi vidhṛtya, yathecchaṃ viharediti

idaṃ kāmyajapa eveti

sarvasiddhiyuto bhūtvā bhairavo vatsarādbhavet

tatastattaddaśāṃśena homayeddhaviṣā priye

rātrau tāmbūlapūrāsyaḥ śayyāyāṃ lakṣamānataḥ

śūdrāṇāñca tathā proktaṃ rātrāviṣṭaṃ mahāphalam

kārayitavyam

ekākṣaro mahāmantraḥ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ

triguṇā tu viśeṣeṇa sarvaśāstraprabodhinī

evaṃ oṃ krāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

anyat sarvaṃ pūrvavat kāryam

caturbāhuyutāṃ devīṃ varābhayakarāṃ smaret

taddaśāṃśavidhānena homayet sādhakottamaḥ

lakṣaṃ rātrau tathā lakṣaṃ mahāśaucaparāyaṇaḥ

rātrau japaikamātreṇa dakṣiṇā siddhidā bhavet

indrasya samīpaṃ aindraṃ rephaḥ

saptabījāni saṃhāraiḥ svāhāntaḥ praṇavādikaḥ

homastu taddaśāṃśataḥ

dhyānapūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ dakṣiṇāvadupācaret

tryakṣarī sā mahāvidyā cāmuṇḍā kālikā smṛtā

asyā jñānaprabhāveṇa kalayāmi jagattrayam

ekāraḥ vahnivallabhā

varṇanyāsakarāṅganyāsau ca dakṣiṇāvat

evaṃ dhyātvā dakṣiṇāvat sarvaṃ kāryam

homayettaddaśāṃśena mṛdupuṣpeṇa mantravit

mahāvidyā mahākālyā mahākālena bhāṣitā

etattrayaṃ vahnivallabhā

nijabījatrayaṃ phaṭ vahnivallabhā

nijabījatrayaṃ kūrcaṃ lajjā punastānyeva vahnivallabhā

hṛdi kālikāyai devatāyai namaḥ

pūrvoktānāṃ mantrāṇāṃ sarvaṃ dakṣiṇāvat kāryam

anyāsāṃ mantravarṇasaṃkhyalakṣajapaḥ

nijabījadvayaṃ māyādvayaṃ dakṣiṇa - kālike vahnivallabhā

nijaṃ kūrcaṃ lajjā dakṣiṇe kālike phaṭ

pūrvaṣaḍbījāni vahnivallabhā

anyat sarvaṃ dakṣiṇāvat

bhairavo'sya ṛṣiḥ

nijabījadvayaṃ kūrcadvayaṃ lajjāyugaṃ vahnivallabhā

asya pañcavaktra ṛṣiḥ

mūlatrayaṃ kūrcadvayaṃ lajjādvayaṃ vahnivallabhā

mūlabījaṃ dakṣiṇe kālike vahnivallabhā

nijabījaṃ kūrcadvayaṃ māyāṃ punastāni vahnivallabhā

hṛdayaṃ vāgbhavaṃ mūladvayaṃ kālikāyai ṭhadvayam

puraścaraṇaṃ lakṣajapaḥ

caturbhujāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāmityādi

ekādaśākṣarī kālī caturvargaphalapradā

dakṣiṇe kālike ceti astrāntā samudīritā

pūrvavatṣaṭ tathā bījānyante ca vahnisundarī

devatā kathitā sadbhiḥ kālī dakṣiṇapūrvikā

bhairavo'sya ṛṣiḥ proktaḥ sarvatantrasamanvitaḥ

svāhāntā kathitā vidyā sarvakāmaphalapradā

pañcākṣarī mahāvidyā pañcavaktra-ṛṣiḥ smṛtaḥ

svāhāntā kathitā vidyā sarvasampatkarī matā

svāhāntā kathitā vidyā sarvaśatrukṣayaṅkarī

mūladvayaṃ kūrcayugmaṃ tathā lajjādvayaṃ tataḥ

caturdaśākṣarī vidyā caturvargaphalapradā

ṣoḍaśīyaṃ samākhyātā vidyā kalpadrumopamā

kālikāyai padaṃ coktvā tadante vahnisundarī

dīrghatanucchadaṃ kālīmanuḥ pañcadaśākṣaraḥ

lakṣasaṃkhyaṃ japaṃ kuryātpuraścaraṇasiddhaye

madhye tu vaindavaṃ cakraṃ bījamāyāvibhūṣitam

guhyakālīṃ mahāvidyāṃ trailokya cātidurlabhām

athavetvi guhye kālike bījadvayaṃ dakṣiṇe kālike vā mantraḥ

asya mantrasyeti śeṣaḥ

dakṣiṇe padamābhāṣya bhavet pañcadaśākṣarī

kāmabījaṃ parityajya ṣoḍaśākṣarī

dakṣiṇe padamābhāṣya bhavedvidyā daśākṣarī

balidānaṃ pūrvavatparikalpayet

oṃ sadāśivamahāpretāya guhyakālyāsanāya namaḥ

caturvargapradā vidyā bhadrakālī śubhāvahā

viṃśatyekādhikā vidyā śmaśānakālikā matā

viṃśatyarṇā mahāvidyā mahākālyāḥ prakīrtitā

viṣṇuṃ śivaṃ tathā sūrya gaṇeśaṃ pūjayettataḥ

prasannavadanāṃ saumyāṃ navaratnavibhūṣitām

yeṣāṃ vijñānamātreṇa jīvanmuktastu sādhakaḥ

pāṇḍityaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu dhanairdhanapatirbhavet

sarvatra jayamāpnoti bṛhaspatirivāparaḥ

raśmipañcakaṃ varṇapañcakamityarthaḥ

ugrāpattāriṇī yasmādugratārā prakīrtitā

tataḥ prabhṛti vidyeyaṃ phaladātrī na kasyacit

mantrasya jñānamātreṇa vijayī bhuvi jāyate

satarīñca maheśāni vadhūbījaṃ prakīrtitam

candraḥ sakāraḥ

sarvabhāṣāmayī śuddhā sarvāmnāyairnamaskṛtā

ekākṣī strīṃ tena sarvatra śāpoddhāraḥ

jīvanīmadhyagā māyā

eṣā pañcākṣarī

anyāsāṃ vidyānāṃ ekajaṭaiva devathā prakṛtitvāt

tatrasthaḥ sādhayedyogī vidyāṃ tribhuvaneśvarīm

tadeva liṅgamākhyātaṃ tatra siddhiranuttamā

devāgāre ca śūnye ca nirjanaikāntaveśmani

dehānte yogimukhyaḥ paramasukhapadaṃ brahmanirvāṇameti

tārābhedairiti ugratāraikajaṭānīlasarasvatībhedaiḥ

sacaturthīpraṇavādinamo'ntakāḥ

svastikādikrameṇa tatropaviśet

bījalikhanantu pūrvavat

nīlatanoḥ paraṃ nigaditaṃ sarvārthasiddhipradam

sarvatra praṇavādinamo'ntena pūjayet

tanmadhye pūjayeddevyā vāhanaṃ śavameva ca

dhyātvā tadupari hūṃkārabhūṣitāṃ kartṛkāṃ dhyāyet

dhārayantīmiti

iti prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ bhavati

hūṃ bījaṃ mantraśaktiḥ syāccaturvargaphalapradā

vāmajaṅghāyām

sarvavāgrūpiṇīmuktvā astrāya phaḍiti smaret

sarvavighnopaśamanīṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm

sarvakāmapradāṃ nityaṃ sarvasāmrajyadāyinīṃ

vadānyāya kulīnāya śuddhācāraratāya ca

evaṃ vidhāya deveśi sādhakāya prakāśayet

anyathā siddhihāniḥ syādityājñā śaṅkaraiḥ kṛtā

ekena sahitā rudrāḥ pañcāśat parikīrtitāḥ

evaṃ dhyātvā tāriṇīntu samāhitamanāściram

iti rudranyāsaḥ

lakṣābhyāṃ gudadeśe ca ketuṃ dhūmraṃ varānane

iti lokapālanyāsaḥ

tataḥ paraśivo devi ṣaṭśivāḥ parikīrtitāḥ

sarvatra tryakṣarīmuktvā vādisāntaṃ savindukam

vādilāntaṃ pravinyasya nābhau tu maṇipūrake

anāhate kādiṭhāntam īśvaraṃ kākinīṃ nyaset

sadāśivaṃ śākinīntu vinyasetpūrvavattataḥ

paraṃ śivaṃ brahmarūpaṃ hākinīsahitaṃ nyaset

iti śivaśaktinyāsaḥ

iti tārādinyāsaḥ

kaṭyāṃ 3 kāñcīpīṭhāya namaḥ

akṣobhyaḥ sarvajantūnāṃ nyāsasyāsya prasādataḥ

kramotkramādvarārohe tārāṣoḍhā prakīrtitā

yoginīnāṃ bhavetpūjyaḥ na devo sa tu mānuṣaḥ

nāstyasya pūjyo lokeṣu pitṛmātṛmukhojjvalaḥ

vindusargānvitāṃ nyasyet ḍādyarṇān sthitivartmanā

sthitestu śāntivinyāsastasmātkāryastridhā mataḥ

sahajānau vanavāsini sthitiñca vidyārthināṃ sṛṣṭim

saṃhṛtiḥ kṣakārādyakārāntaḥ

praṇavaṃ mātṛkāvarṇeḥ puṭitaṃ mātṛkāsthale

māyābījaṃ tathā devi vinyastavyaṃ prayatnataḥ

kūrcabījaṃ tathā devi nyasanīyamaśeṣataḥ

avaśyaṃ pratyahaṃ kāryā na pūjā na japastathā

tataḥ puṣpāñjaliṃ viracyātmābhedena devatāṃ dhyāyet

samāpayet

ratnapādukayornyastapādāmbujayugāṃ smaret

daśāṃśaṃ juhuyānmantrī ghṛtāktai raktapuṣpakaiḥ

dhyānapūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavacca samācaret

māye svāheti mantro'yaṃ proktaḥ saptadaśākṣaraḥ

japamasyāścaredyastu sa syādadrutakavistathā

eṣā aṣṭākṣarī vidyā kathitā bhuvi durlabhā

anayorjapapūjādīn pañcākṣarīvadācaret

tadante yadi phaṭkāro manuḥ pañcākṣaro bhavet

vadhūbījamaparo'sau guṇākṣaraḥ

athavānyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamucyate

nikṣipya bhūmau hastārddhamānataḥ kānane vane

ekākī prajapenmantrī sa bhavet kalpapādapaḥ

sa bhavet sarvasiddhīśo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

tāvajjaptvā nirātaṅkaḥ sarvasiddhiśvaro bhavet

aṣṭamyādinavamyantamupavāsaparo bhavet

sahasrasaṃkhyajaptena puraścaraṇamiṣyate

navamīrātrau pūjāṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ

ṣaṭasahasraṃ japennityaṃ bhaktibhāvaparāyaṇaḥ

kevalaṃ japamātreṇa mantrāḥ siddhā bhavanti hi

bhavettāvajjaptvāmaheśāni puraścaraṇamiṣyate

sādhayet svahitaṃ mantrī mantradhyānaparāyaṇaḥ

caturdaśiṃ na vīkṣeta mantrameva samabhyaset

samāmaguṇasampannaiḥ sādhayedvītabhīḥ svayam

anyānapi ca rakṣārthaṃ kiñciddūre niveśayet

cāṇḍālādiṣu saṃprāptā kevalaṃ śīghrasiddhidā

mahāsvaccho dayāvāṃśca sarvabhūtahite rataḥ

tatastaistaiḥ suhṛdvargai rakṣito nābhibhūyate

tadadhaścāstramantreṇa nikhanetkuladīpakam

bhūtvā japenmantramananyadhīḥ

punardevatā sampūjya japet

satyantu kārayitvā ca varayedvaramuttamam

baliñca yavāpiṣṭamayena

dināntare'pi dāsyāmi svīkṛtya svagṛhaṃ vrajet

piṣṭeneti yavadhānyodbhavenetyarthaḥ

candrahāsena vidhivattattanmantreṇa ghātayet

vilvamūle śmaśāne vā tatsamīpe vanasthale

bhaumavāre tamisrāyāṃ sādhayet siddhimuttamām

tilāḥ kuśāḥ sarṣaṃpāśca sthāpanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ

ghātaya hūṃ phaṭ oṃ sahasrāre hūṃ phaṭ

vikīrya, vihitaśavasamīpaṃ gacchet

strījanañcedṛśaṃ rūpaṃ sarvathā parivarjayet

punaḥ prakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā japasthāne samānayet

catuḥṣaṣṭiyoginībhyo ḍākinībhyo'pi saṃdiśet

atra śmaśānasādhanakrameṇa japaḥ kāryaḥ

śavaṃ jale tu garte vā nikṣipya srānamācaret

tataḥ snātvā ca bhuktvā ca nivaseduttamasthale

caṇḍograśūlapāṇeśca mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ

mātaṅgī devatā devī sarvakāryapradāyinī

ekonaviṃśatyarṇo'yaṃ sarvābhīṣṭakaro bhavet

yāṃ yāṃ prārthayate siddhiṃ haṭhāttāṃ tāmavāpnuyāt

valimapi ucchiṣṭena

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya vahiḥpūjāmārabhet

punardhyātvāvāhya pañcāpacāraiḥ pūjayet

lakṣe japte mantrasiddhiṃ puraścaryāṃ samācaret

etenaiva susiddhaḥ syāt puraścaryādiko vidhiḥ

yaḥ smareddevi vidyāṃ tāṃ dāridryairnābhibhūyate

tena devyāṃ mahāprītirvāñchitārthaṃ dadāti ca

sarvālaṅkāramutsṛjya dattvā yāti nijālayam

purayitvā maheśāni vaśagā jāyate śubhā

dātavyaṃ sarvadā tasmai nityaṃ dāridryaśāntaye

naivedyañca pradātavyaṃ nityaṃ dāridryaśāntaye

tasya dāridryasaṃnyāsaṃ dāsīvat karavāṇyaham

homo'pi ca vidhātavyaḥ kṣaṇāddāridryaśāntaye

tāmrapātre tathā kāryaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sumanoharam

kuto dāridryaśaṅkāsya sa hi koṭīśvaro bhavet

kuberasya matenāsyāḥ pūjādi kriyate sadā

pūrvādi - uttarāntañca madhye devīṃ prapūjayet

hṛdayādiṣu

nānālaṅkārabhūṣāṅgīṃ nagnāṃ mattāṃ sadāsavaiḥ

nagno bhūtvā mahāpūjāṃ kuryādrātrau viśeṣataḥ

tadvahirasitāṅgādibhairavān pūjayet

varṇalakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ taddaśāṃśena homayet

kālikā devyā iti nijabījena ityarthaḥ

yasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa pavano'pi sthirāyate

vahnihīnendrayuṅmāyā sthiramāyā prakīrtitā

ityapi mantrāntaram

hlīṃ bījaṃ guhyadeśe tu svāhāśaktistu pādayoḥ

karaśākhāsu talayoḥ karāṅganyāsamācaret

krameṇa mantravarṇāṃstu nyasya dhyāyedyathāvidhi

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya bahiḥpūjāmārabhet

pūjopakaraṇañcābhyukṣayet

tarjanyāṃguṣṭhayogena sāṅgāvaraṇāṃ bagalāmukhī tarpayet

priyaṃgukusumenāpi pītapuṣpaiśca homayet

pītabhūṣaṇabhūṣāñca ratnasiṃhāsane sthitām

japahomaprayoge ca mantraṃ cāpyayutaṃ japet

stambhayetparasainyāni nātra kāryā vicāraṇa

stambhaneṣu ca sarveṣu prayogaḥ pratyayāvahaḥ

etā daśa mahāvidyā siddhavidyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

labhate'cireṇa niyataṃ paiśācikī bhaktitaḥ

piṇḍaṃ piśāci svāheti nṛpārṇaḥ kathitaḥ priye

sarvajñāṃ śavahṛtkṛtādhivasatīṃ paiśācikīṃ tāṃ numaḥ

dagdhamīnaṃ baliṃ dattvā rātrau sampūjya saṃjapet

svāheti dagdhamīnabaliṃ dadyāt

amṛtaṃ kuru deveśi svāheti prokṣayejjalaiḥ

mūlaṃ raktacandanena likhitvā yantropari iṣṭadevatāṃ pūjayet

siddhirbhaviṣyatīti jñātvā tathā vidhamācaret

tataḥ sarvajño bhavati nāsya pūjādhyānam

bhūtabhaviṣyādikaṃ paśyati

śrīmañjughoṣo jayatāṃ sādhakānāṃ sukhāvahaḥ

īyantu dīpanī proktā mūlamantrastu kathyate

vidyā śrutidharī proktā eṣā varṇatrayātmikā

sarvajñavidyeyamekarṇātmikā priye

likheccandanalekhanyā prayatnāt sādhakottamaḥ

kuce śrutidharo nityaṃ gomuṇḍe ca mahākaviḥ

varaṃ prāpya ca tasmādvai viharet yathāsukham

tribhirmāsairbhavenmarttyaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ

vakeśaḥ śakāraḥ

mañjughoṣaṃ namāmi

sarvasiddhi pradāyeti puṣpaṃ dadyād vicakṣaṇaḥ

kapālī bhīṣaṇaścaiva saṃhāraścāṣṭamaḥ smṛtaḥ

pūjā etāstu yoginyo hṛllekhābījapūrvikāḥ

evaṃ siddhamanurmantrī mahāyogīśvaro bhavet

śaṃkaraṃ paripapraccha pārvatī parameśvaram

vāñchitārthapradaṃloke mañjughoṣaṃ bravīhi me

bhaktānāṃ kāmadaṃ mantraṃ kalpavṛkṣamivāparam

yajjñātvā jaḍadhīḥ prāyo vācaspati samobhavet

japāt siddhipradaṃ mantraṃ vinā homārcanādibhiḥ

sarvajñatvamavāpnoti satyaṃ satyaṃ hi pārvatī

sarvaśāstreṣu so'pyuccairbṛhaspatisamo bhavet

dhyeyaḥ ko vātra tatsarvaṃ brūhi me bhaktavatsala

sa eva maṃjughoṣākhyo bhaktidānena muktidaḥ

tadā muktiprado mantro nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

yathā dhyātvā japenmantraṃ tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu

dhyānaṃ vakṣye maheśāni krameṇa hitakāmyayā

siddhipradaṃ sādhakānāṃ bhaktānāṃ cintitapradam

aprakāśyaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit

vasupradaḥ syāt

balinaivedyabhuk sākṣādbṛhaspatirivāparaḥ

māsatrayeṇa satataṃ kavireva na saṃśayaḥ

yantre mantraṃ likhedādau paścānmantraṃ japet punaḥ

nirjanaṃ sthānamāgatya japennanmatramadhomukhaḥ

aṣṭādhikaiśca saṃpūjya japenmantraṃ catuṣpathe

bṛhaspatisamo vaktā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

trimuṇḍametadvikhyātaṃ sādhakānāṃ sukhāvaham

dakṣiṇe ca śivāmuṇḍaṃ kṛtvā pūjāṃ samācaret

vṛṣamastakasthaḥ

navakuṃkumarocanābhiḥ

varakhaḍgahastam

ariṣṭagehe niśi tailamevamādāya yatnātkarapallavena

ākiṃśukākṣoḍataroścamūle, vilipya pādau vadanāmṛtena

sākṣādgīṣpatirnātra citram

vākpatirīśvaraḥ syāt

gīramṛtāyamānā

juhuyādyatnato mantrī sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt

navagīṣpatiḥ syāt

dhanavaṃśapradaṃ ramyaṃ sārvajñavāgmitāpradam

adoṣakavitāmūlaṃ sarvatra pratibhāpradam

maṃjughoṣaḥ sa kaḥ kīdṛktasyānuṣṭhānameva hi

maṃjughoṣastu yo devaḥ so'haṃ devi na saṃśayaḥ

tasyānuṣṭhānamadhunā śrūyatāṃ mama tattvataḥ

rasalakṣāvadhistasya jāpya eva surepsitaḥ

sukavitvaṃ bhavet tasya pratibhā viśvajitvarī

ṣaṇmāsaṃ yastujapati sa sarvajñaḥ kuśāgradhīḥ

tuṇḍakīnāladānena bhavedācamanīyakam

maṃjughoṣaṃ namāmi

śṛṇu devi mahābhāge kalau sadyaḥ phalapradam

taṃ namāmi hitaṃ nāthaṃ maṃjughoṣaṃ namāmyaham

raktaṃ rajoguṇairyuktaṃ maṃjughoṣaṃ namāmyaham

taṃ śāntaṃ tamasāyuktaṃ pītavastraṃ namāmyaham

caraṇe patito jīvo buddhaye taṃ namāmyaham

hṛṣṭaṃ samastasārañca maṃjughoṣaṃ namāmyaham

sakalaṃ niṣphalañcaiva taṃ namāmi hitapradam

dakṣiṇāṃ śaktito dadyādgurutuṣṭiryathā bhavet

yāvat kārla suto duḥkhī pitā tu narakaṃ vrajet

caturvargaphalā vidyā sarvasiddhipradāyikā

navākṣaro manurdavi tāriṇyāḥ samudīritaḥ

aṣṭasiddhipradā devī caturvargaphalapradā

anayā sadṛśī siddhirna bhūtā na bhaviṣyati

binā chando binā śikṣāṃ binābhyāsena pārvati

jihvāyāṃ jāyate tasya kavitvaṃ rasanirmitam

dvirācamya śikhāṃ baddhā tato bhūmiviśodhanam

karau saṃśodhya deveśi cāstramantreṇa tatparam

tadudbhutāgnibalibhiḥ śarīraṃ dahyate budhaiḥ

śarīrasya viśodhena prāṇāyāmaṃ caret tataḥ

mantranyāsastato devi śrūyatāṃ mantrasiddhidaḥ

pādayoḥ vāgbhavaśaktaye namaḥ

tathā bījena tu ṣaḍaṅgāni prakalpayet

kālī gaurī śivā caiva ityaṣṭau śaktayaḥ smṛtāḥ

dehapīṭhe tato devīṃ dhyāyetsusthiramānasaḥ

candrasūryāgnibhedena trilocana-samanvitām

caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ aṣṭavajrasamanvitam

raktapuṣpairmahādevi nānāgandhasamanvitaiḥ

vedavidyāprakāśastu tanme brūhi jagatpate

etadeva purā pṛṣṭhaṃ kalpādau viṣṇave mayā

yatproktaṃ tena me brahman tatte vakṣyāmi yatnataḥ

yasyā vijñānamātreṇa jāḍyāpaharaṇaṃ bhavet

abhyāsācca bhavedyasya vācaścitrā bhavanti hi

dvaipāyano'pi yaṃ jñātvā vedavyāso'bhavanmuniḥ

japed dvādaśalakṣantu mūko'pi vākpatirbhavet

tasyopari punardhyāyed devīṃ vāgīśvarīṃ tataḥ

ātmābhedena dhyātvaivaṃ tataḥ sampūjayet kramāt

āvāhya pūjayet tasyāṃ devīṃ vāgīśvarīṃ tataḥ

evaṃ sampūjayenmantrī japapūjārataḥ sadā

na vismarati medhāvī śrutān vedāgamānapi

bhīmāṃ saṃprāpya vāksiddhiṃ kavīnāmagraṇīrbhavet

tatprabhāpaṭalaṃ vyāptaṃ jagatsarvaṃ vicintayet

kavitvaṃ jñānaśaktyā tu pāṇḍityamadhikaṃ bhavet

evaṃ kṛtvā vidhānantu sākṣādvaiśravaṇo bhavet

padmahomena mahatīṃ prāpnuyātśriyamurjjitām

sneha bhāvena samproktaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit

viṣṇunā dattamasmabhyaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ dvijottama

gadyapadyamayīṃ brāhmīṃ siddhavidyāprasādaḥ

bhaktibhāvena te devi kathayāmi śucismite

kavitāmatulaiśvaryaṃ dadāti padamuttamam

nṛpāṇāṃ vaśyatā caiva jāyate ca tathā priye

ṅeyutā caṇḍikā caiva mantraḥ prokto daśākṣaraḥ

ādāvaṅgāni sampūjya śastrapūjā tataḥ param

dikṣu pūjyā imā devyaḥ susiddhāḥ phaladāyikāḥ

evaṃ sañcintayed devīṃ jāyate narapuṅgavaḥ

bhujaṅgeṣu yathā tārkṣyaḥ krūrakāryo yathā śaniḥ

idanta paramaṃ guhyaṃ saṃkṣepātkathitaṃ mayā

palāyante mahādevi sādhvasena kṣaṇāttataḥ

taṃ dṛṣṭvā puruṣaṃ devi hṛtkṣobho jāyate ripoḥ

palāyante mahādevi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

sahasratritayaṃ hutvā vāgīśo jāyate'cirāt

durbhagā ca bhavetpatyuḥ subhagātimanoramā

nīlotpalaiḥ sarojairvā hutvā vai śravaṇāyate

devi sa ripūnnāśayeddhruvam

harirvā śaṃkaro vāpi na śakto rakṣituṃ kvacit

umābījaṃ hṛllekhābījaṃ cāmuṇḍābījaṃ śyāmābījam

acchedyābhedyakāyaḥ syāt ripūṇāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ

iti kātyāyanīkalpaḥ

yasyāḥ prasādamātreṇa bhaved gaṅgādharaḥ svayam

ekākṣarīsamā nāsti vidyā tribhuvane priye

vināyāsairmahādevi japamātreṇa siddhidā

caturvargapradā durgā sarvasattveṣu saṃsthitā

tryakṣarī vividhā vidyā kathitā brahmaṇā purā

kālīvadācaredvidyāṃ japedvidyāmaharniśam

prajapetparayā bhaktyā durgāṃ durgatināśinīm

pūjayetparayā bhaktyā durgā durgatihāriṇīm

nānāmāṃsairnānādravyaiḥ pūjayet parameśvarīm

tadā bhavenmahāsiddhirnātra kāryā vicāraṇā

praphullakamalārūḍhāṃ dhyāyet tāṃ bhavagehinīm

prabhādyāḥ śaktayaḥ pūjyā gandhādyairnavakoṇake

suprabhā vijayā sarvasiddhidā nava śaktayaḥ

prasaṅgāt kathitā vidyā trailokyadurlabhā priye

dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ

vākyantu oṃ hrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

evaṃ dhyātvā mahādevīmupacāraiḥ prapūjayet

tato yathāśakti japtvā pūrvavacca samācarediti

dviṭhāntaḥ ṣoḍaśārṇo'yaṃ mantraḥ sadbhirudāhṛta

hrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

pīṭhamanvantaṃ sampūjya, punardhyātvāvāhya, devīṃ pūjayet

tato dhūpādivisarjanāntaṃ karma samāpayet

daśāṃśaṃ homayedājyaiḥ puraścaraṇasiddhaye

yasmai kasmai dīyate sa vaśyo bhavati niścitam

etat pūjādikaṃ saubhāgyavaśyasarvasampatkaram

sarvastrīpuruṣavaśaṅkari suyuddhadurghorāṃ hrīṃ svāhā

suyuddhadurghorarāve hrīṃ svāhā astrāya phaṭ

asyāṅgairlokapālairmātṛkābhistrīṇyāvaraṇāni

japantribhuvanavaśyakaraḥ

japādau sarvajanasthāne sādhyanāma deyamiti

iṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

evaṃ dhyātvāvāhya pūjayet

tato vajrādīnpūjayet

ājyena tilairayuta homaḥ

paramā śrīrbhavati

viṣamālokanenaiva hanyānnāgakulodbhavam

praṇavo bījaṃ, svāhā śaktiḥ

hṛdayādiṣvevam

pādakaṭihṛdayavaktramūrddhasu pañcavarṇānnyaset

pūjayet

ghṛtāktaiḥ kṛṣṇapuṣpairdaśāṃśahomaḥ

svāhā

evaṃ dhyānamātreṇa daṣṭo nirviṣyaḥ samutthāya ciraṃ jīvet

iti garuḍastotram

kiñcidanyat tu devānāṃ sādhanaṃ yadi kathyatām

tatsādhanavidhiṃ vakṣye nṛṇāṃ siddhikaraṃ drutam

jayena sādhanaṃ kṛtvā jitaṃ sarvaṃ carācaram

ekāgracittamādhāya sādhayet sādhanaṃ mahat

lakṣajapāt prasannaḥ syāt satyaṃ te kathitaṃ mayā

divārātro japaṃ kuryād yāvat sandarśanaṃ bhavet

suprasannastato bhūtvā prayāti sādhakāgrataḥ

varaṃ labdhvā sādhakendro viharedātmanaḥ sukham

iti garuḍatantre devīśvarasaṃvāde hanūmatsādhanam

mūlamantraṃ tato japtvā siñcedādityasaṃkhyayā

karāṅganyāsau kuryāt

iti dhyātvā ṣaṭsahasraṃ japet

daśārṇo'yaṃ manuḥ prokto narāṇāṃ surapādapaḥ

tatkṣaṇādeva cāpnoti satyaṃ satyaṃ suniścitam

sa ca visargabinduyuktaḥ khakāradvayasvarūpaḥ

pādayoḥ binduśaktaye namaḥ

dhyānārcane śāradoktavaiśvānaramantravat

sarvavedanāñca nāśayet

iti mūṣikaviṣaharamantraḥ

iti mūṣikavināśanamantraḥ

iti durgāmantro lūtāviṣaharaḥ

iti viṣṇumantraḥ sarvakīṭajātiviṣaharaḥ

ityārdrapaṭī

manorathān pūraya hūṃ phaṭ svāhā

nyāsaśuddhyādikaṃ kiñcinnātra kāryā vicāraṇā

dhyātvaivaṃ sādhakaḥ sādhyaṃ sādhayenmanasi sthitam

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ kumbhasthāne sthitā imāḥ

brāhmyādyaṣṭaśaktīḥ pūrvādikrameṇa pūjayet

saptāhādvairiṇaṃ hanti kālīmantraprayogataḥ

dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi sthitvā grāmāderdakṣiṇāmukhaḥ

oṃ namo hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

smṛtimātreṇa vai mantrī ripūna sarvān vināśayet

vā nikhanet, sa unmatto bhavati

vā nikhanet tasya samastaparivārā naśyanti

tena śāntirbhavati

sahasreṇābhimantritaṃ yasya gehe nikhanet, sa jvareṇa vinaśyati

vā nikhanet, tasya sarvānnāśayati

dattaṃ tasya samvidā nottame nāke aghoroha vairam

tuṣṭupchando bandhanādivyasanaparihāre viniyogaḥ

tato bandhanādvyasanācca mukto bhavati nānyathā

saptabhirdivasairbhūpān vaśayedvidhināmunā

vaśyo bhavati sādhyaśca nātra kāryā vicāraṇā

vaśyāya sarvajantunāṃ prayogo'yamudāhṛtaḥ

urvārukamiva bandhanānmṛtyormukṣīyamāmṛtāt

vibhaktairmantravarṇaiśca ṣaḍaṅgānāñca kalpanam

amukasyāmukaśāntaye viniyogaḥ

pāṇyornāsikayoḥ śīrṣe mantravarṇānnyasetkramāt

guhyorujānupadeṣu nyāsamevaṃ samācaret

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasaiḥ sampūjya arghyasthāpanaṃ kuryāt

vahnaye vāyave viyate yajamānāya

daugdhaṃ dugdhaṃ dadhi punardūrvāstāni vidurbudhāḥ

sākṣānmṛtyorvimucyeta kimanyāḥ kṣudrikāḥ kriyāḥ

ādhivyādhivinirmukto dīrghamāyuravāpnuyāt

āmayānakhilāñjitvā dīrghamāyuravāpnuyāt

dhanadhānyasamṛddhiḥ syādacireṇaiva sidhyati

asya japātsarvasiddhirbhavati

dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt mṛtyormukṣīyamāmṛtāt

asya japāt savasiddhibhavati

yaddṛṣṭaṃ tripurātantre yaddṛṣṭaṃ bhūtaḍāmare

svāhāntaṃ mantra muddhṛtya japed daśasahasrakam

ākarṣayenna sandehaḥ kiṃ punarmānuṣīmiha

yāvadāyāti santrastā madanālasavigrahā

cāmuṇḍā śavavāhinī japavidhau dhyeyā sadā sādhakaiḥ

yayornāma tayoḥ śīghraṃ vidveṣañca parasparam

yasya nāma bhavet tasya vidveṣañca parasparam

mantrantu saṃpravakṣyāmi mahābhairavasaṃjñakam

etanmantraṃ likhettatra vidveṣo jāyate dhruvam

jñānamātreṇa śatrūṇāṃ vidveṣo jāyate dhruvam

bhasmanā tena yaṃ hanyādvidveṣastadbhavennṛṇām

yadā mithyā bhaveddevi yogarājaḥ sudurlabhaḥ

pūrvadravyeṇa vidveṣaṃ kārayedatha sādhakaḥ

parasparaṃ bhaveddveṣī yogo'yaṃ kubjikāmate

dhyānayuktena manasā śatroruccāṭanaṃ bhavet

evaṃ vidhaṃ dhiyā bhāvyaṃ kāryamuccāṭanaṃ ripoḥ

gopanīyaṃ prayatnena na prakāśyaṃ kadācana

hūṃ amukaṃ hana hana svāhā

mūlamantreṇa kuryāditi

tataḥ sukhaprasavobhavati

mantrayitvāñjayennetre devairapi na dṛśyate

dattvā, trailokyādṛśyo bhavati

atiguhyā mahāvidyā devānāmapi durlabhā

asyā abhyarcanenaiva rājatvaṃ labhate naraḥ

kṛtvā ca prajapenmantraṃ niśīthe yāti sundarī

sucandanaṃ sumanaso dattvābhilaṣitaṃ vadet

bhūpatitvaṃ prārthitaṃ yat tad dadāti dine dine

bhātṛvat pālitaṃ loke kāmanābhirmanogataiḥ

tatsarvaṃ sādhakendrāya nivedayati niścitam

anyastrīgamanaṃ tyaktvā anyathā naśyati dhruvam

pūrvavat sakalakāryaṃ candanairmaṇḍalaṃ likhet

gandhaṃ puṣparasañcaiva tāmbūlādīṃśca mūlataḥ

varaṃ varaya śīghraṃ rtva yatte manasi varttate

prāṇāyāmaṃ ṣaḍaṅgañca māyayā ca samācaret

tato'rcitā prasannā sa puṣṇāti prārthitañca yat

sa cāśeṣaṃ vyayaṃ kuryāt sthite tattuna dāsyati

avyāhatagatistasya bhavatīti na saṃśayaḥ

tava snehena bhaktyā ca vaddho'haṃ parameśvari

prāṇāyāmaṃ ṣaḍaṅgañca māyayātha samācaret

arghyamucchiṣṭaraktena dadyāt tasyai dine dine

kāyena mamasā vācā pūjayecca dine dine

āniśīthaṃ japenmantraṃ baliṃ dattvā manoharam

sādhakendro'pi tāṃ dṛṣṭvā dadyādarghyādikaṃ tataḥ

vastrabhūṣādikaṃ tyaktvā yāti sā nijamandiram

ātmabhāryāṃ parityajya bhajet tāñca vicakṣaṇaḥ

vahnerbhāryā mahāmantraḥ sādhakānāṃ sukhāvahaḥ

evaṃ dhyātvā japenmantraṃ niśīthe yāti sā tadā

strībhāvena tadā tasyai dadyāt pādyādikaṃ tataḥ

annādyai ratibhogena pativat pālayeta sadā

evaṃ pratidinam tasya siddhiḥ syāt kāmarūpataḥ

vahnijāyāṣṭasahastryaṃ japenmantraṃ dine-dine

jñātvā dṛḍhaṃ sādhakendraṃ niśīthe yāti niścitam

susantuṣṭā sādhakendraṃ toṣayed ratibhojanaiḥ

tyaktvā bhāryāṃ bhajetāñca anyathā naśyati dhruvam

sarvatra kuśalaṃ jñātvā yāti sā sādhakālayam

tyaktvā bhāryāṃ bhajet tāñca sādhakendraḥ sadā priye

jñātvā yāṃ sādhitā vidyā balā cātibalā priye

mahāvidyeti kathitāṃ gopanīyā prayatnataḥ

mūlamantreṇa sakalaṃ kuryācca susamāhitaḥ

evaṃ dhyātvā japenmantraṃ sahasrañca dine dine

gandhacandanatāmbūlaṃ dadyāt tasyai sadā budhaḥ

sudṛḍhaṃ sādhakaṃ matvā yāti sā sādhakālayam

kṛtvā santoṣayed bhaktyā naṭinī tatatkarotyalam

svarṇaśataṃ siddhidravyaṃ dadāti sā dine dine

atītānāgatāṃ vārttāṃ sarvāṃ jānāti sādhakaḥ

yadyadvāñchati tatsarvaṃ dadāti nātra saṃśayaḥ

jīvanyāsādikaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyet tatra prasannadhīḥ

evaṃ dhyātvā japenmantraṃ sahasrantu dine dine

dhūpadīpavidhānaiśca trisandhyaṃ pūjayenmudā

prabhātasamaye yāti sādhakasyāntikaṃ dhruvam

carvyaṃ coṣyādikaṃ dravyaṃ divyaṃ dadāti sā dhruvam

ānīya dīyate satyaṃ sādhakāśānurūpataḥ

sādhakāya varaṃ datvā yāti sā nijamandiram

reme sārddhaṃ tayā devī sādhakendro dine dine

vahnibhāryā manuḥ proktaḥ sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ

guhyādguhya hyeṣā tava snehātprakīrtitā

athādhikāriṇaḥ ke vā samāsena vada prabho

sadā dhyānaparo bhhūtvā taddarśanamahotsukaḥ

anena vidhinā sākṣādbhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ

iti yoginī sādhana prakaraṇam

yantrañca pratimāsthānamarcane sarvadā hareḥ

kāryā na tu kevalabhūtale

kiṃ punaścārcanaṃ tatra harisānnidhyakarāṇam

maṇḍalādīnāntu sarvasādhāraṇatvāt

vā śilāsthāṃ vā pūjayet parameśvarīm

etat sarvaṃ yantrābhāve

tathādau vilikhed yantraṃ devatāyāśca vigraham

yantramityāhuretasmin devaḥ prīṇāti pūjitaḥ

iti yantrasya pūjayāṃ prādhānyamiti

idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi pratiṣṭhākarmanirṇayam

tasyodyāpanakarmāṅgaṃ sarvavarṇavinirṇayam

toyadhūpāntaraiḥ kuryāt pañcāmṛta vidhiṃ budhaḥ

kaṣāyajalasampūṛnaiḥ kārayet snānamuttamam

aṣṭottaraśataṃ devi devatābhāvasiddhaye

phalatāmbūlanaivedyairdevīṃ tatra samarcayet

sarvametat prayatnena dadyādātmahite rataḥ

balidānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā praṇamedyantrarājakam

homakarmaṇya śaktaśced dviguṇaṃ japamācaret

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt tato devyā visarjanam

dhanadhānya-putra-pautra-yaśa-āyūṃṣi muñcati

yasya vai mamatā devi putra-pautragṛhādiṣu

sarvatra snānaṃ mūlamatreṇa

viniyogaḥ

iti prāṇān pratiṣṭhāpya devīmāvāhyet tataḥ

tato dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā acchidrāvadhāraṇaṃ kuryāt

tantrasāre tṛtīyaḥ paricchedaḥ

śuddhāsanaṃ samāsādya bhūtotsāraṇamācaret

baddhapadmāsano mantrī samāhitajitendriyaḥ

rātro pūjane nāyaṃ nimayaḥ

śivārcanaṃ sadāpyevaṃ śuciḥ kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ

śaktidvayaṃ tathā sūryadvayamekatra nārcayet

eteṣāmarcanānnityamudvegaṃ prāpnuyādgṛhī

pratyekenāthavā devi bilvapatraiḥ prapūjayet

śālagrāmaśilāmekāṃ gṛhastho'pi prapūjayet

na dūrvayā yajed durgāṃ bilvapatrairdivākaram

unmattamarkapuṣpañca viṣṇorvarjya sadā budhaiḥ

vilvapatraistatprasūnairnārcayeddevakīsutam

iti yadbilvavidhānaṃ tadvihitapuṣpābhāve

ghusṛṇaṃ kumudaṃ raktaṃ hayāriñca visarjayet

vāsobhiḥ saṃbhṛtaṃ nītaṃ matimānnārcayecchive

svayaṃ vikasitairiti puruṣeṇa svayaṃ vikāsitairityarthaḥ

bhūgataṃ varjayet puṣpaṃ śephālīṃ vakulaṃ vinā

tamālasya ca padmasya chinnabhinne na dūṣyati

dinaikaṃ karavīrāṇi yogyāni ca tapodhana

nirmālyabhūtaiḥ kusumairucchiṣṭaiḥ parameśvari

tulasīghrāṇamātreṇa kruddhā bhavati sundarī

mahādevyai yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ

nārcayet tu mahādevīṃ kuśakāśodbhavena ca

na dūṣyecchinna-bhinnañca jātīpuṣpañca śaṅkari

bilvairmarūvakādyaiśca tulasīdalavarjitaiḥ

iti tathāpi tānniyamastu na tripurādau

itarāsāntuṃ pūjāyāṃ tadasattve sattve vā na doṣaḥ

niṣedhaparam

na tvarghyādiniṣedhaparam

evaṃ na dūrvayā yajed durgāmityādi

iti śrīkramīyavākyāt

kartavyā sarvadevānāṃ bhaktiyogo'tra kāraṇam

idantu vihitapuṣpābhāve atyanta bhaktiviṣayam

apāmārgaiśca bhṛṅgaiśca tulasīvarjitaiḥ śubhaiḥ

ityādi puṣpaniyamo boddhavyaḥ

tatra setuṃ vinā japasya vyarthatvāttannirūpyate

mantrastathaiva niḥsetuḥ kṣaṇāt kṣarati yajvanām

sa cānusvāranādābhyāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ seturucyate

pañcaśuddhiṃ vinā pūjā abhicārāya kalpate

ṣaḍaṅgādyakhilanyāsairātmaśuddhirudīritā

pañcavarṇarajobhiśca sthānaśuddhiritīritā

kramotkramād dvirāvṛttyā mantraśuddhiritīritā

darśayeddhenumudrādīn dravyaśuddhiḥ prakīrtitā

pañcaśuddhiṃ vidhāyetthaṃ paścātpūjāṃ samācaret

punastenaiva karttavyaṃ tataḥ siddho bhaveddhruvam

punastenaiva karttavyaṃ tataḥ siddho na saṃśayaḥ

upāyāstatra karttavyaḥ sapta śaṃkarabhāṣitāḥ

dahanāntaṃ kramāt kuryāt tataḥ siddho bhavenmanuḥ

uśīracandanābhyāntu mantraṃ saṃgrathitaṃ likhet

pūjanājjapanāddhomātbhrāmitaḥ siddhido bhavet

evaṃ ruddho bhavet siddho na cedetadvaśīkuru

dhāryaṃ kaṇṭhe bhavet siddhiḥ pīḍanaṃ vāsya kārayet

pīḍito lajjayāviṣṭaḥ siddhaḥ syādatha poṣayet

poṣito'yaṃ bhavet siddho na cetkurvīta śoṣaṇam

śoṣitaścāpyasiddhaśceddahanīyo'gnibījataḥ

kaṇṭhadeśe tato mantraḥ siddhaḥ syācchaṅkaroditam

ekenaiva kṛtārthaḥ syādbahubhiḥ kimu suvrate

niścitaṃ mantrasiddhiḥ syādityuktaṃ tantravedibhiḥ

prayogo'syākleśasiddhiḥ siddhestu lakṣaṇaṃ param

ityādiguṇasampattirmadhyasiddhestu lakṣaṇam

siddhamantrastu yaḥ sākṣāt sa śivo nātra saṃśayaḥ

ārādhitā mahāvidyā vīryahīnābhavat tadā

śareṇa pīḍitā pūrvaṃ bhuvaneśyāḥ pratiṣṭhitā

mayā pratiṣṭhitā vidyā tārā candrasvarūpiṇī

chinnā ruddhā ca vṛddhā ca nirvījā śaktihīnakā

ativṛddhaḥ pīḍitaśca vakṣyāmyeṣāñca lakṣaṇam

caturddhā pañcadhā vāpi sa mantraśchinnasaṃjñakaḥ

ruddhamantraḥ sa vijñeyo bhūktimuktivivarjitaḥ

śaktihīnaḥ sa kathito yasya madhye na varttate

asau parāṅmukhaḥ prokto hakāro bindusaṃyutaḥ

netrahīnaḥ sa vijñeyo duḥkha śokāmayapradaḥ

māyā namāmi ca padaṃ nāsti yasmin sa kīlitaḥ

na vidyete sa mantrastu stambhitaḥ siddhivarjitaḥ

saptadhā dṛśyate tantu dagdhamantraṃ pracakṣate

srastaḥ sa kathito mantraḥ sarvasiddhivivarjitaḥ

śivo vā śaktirathavā bhītākhyaḥ sa prakīrttitaḥ

sa eva malino mantraḥ sarvavighna samanvitaḥ

astraṃ tiṣṭhati mantraḥ sa tiraskṛta udāhṛtaḥ

sa eva bhedito mantraḥ sarvaśāstravivarjitaḥ

trivarṇo haṃsahīno yaḥ suṣupta udāhṛtaḥ

tadvadastraṃ sthitaṃ madhye yasya mantraḥ sa mūrcchitaḥ

virāme'ṅgasya ye mantro hṛtavīrya sa ucyate

jñātavyo bhīma ityeva yaḥ syādaṣṭādaśākṣaraḥ

hṛllekhāṃkuśabījāḍhyaḥ pradhvastastaṃ pracakṣate

ṣoḍaśārṇo yuvā prauḍhaścatvāriṃśallipimanuḥ

catuḥśatākṣaraścapi vṛddhaḥ sa parikīrttitaḥ

śivaśaktyarṇahīno vā sa nirbīja udāhṛtaḥ

sa mantraḥ siddhihīnaḥ syātmandaḥ paṃktyakṣaro manuḥ

dvivarṇaḥ sattvahīnaḥ syāccaturvavarṇastu kekaraḥ

sārddhadvādaśavarṇo'pi dhūmitaḥ satu ninditaḥ

viṃśatyarṇastriṃśadarṇo yaḥ syādāliṅgitaḥ smṛtaḥ

kṣudhāttaḥ sa tu vijñeyo caturviṃśativarṇakaḥ

trayoviṃśativarṇo vā mantro dṛpta udāhṛtaḥ

triṃśadekonavarṇo vā tvaṅgahīnaḥ sa eva hi

atikruddhaḥ sa vijñeyo ninditaḥ sarvakarmasu

atikrūraḥ sa vijñeyo ninditaḥ sarvakarmasu

tāvatsaṃkhyā nigaditā mantrāḥ savrīḍasaṃjñakāḥ

te sarve kathitā mantrāḥ sthānabhraṣṭā na śobhanāḥ

te sarve vikalā jñeyāḥ śataṃ sārddhaṃ śatantathā

yāvat śatatrayaṃ saṃkhyā niḥsnehāste prakīrttitāḥ

ativṛddhaḥ sa mantrastu sarvaśāstravivarjitaḥ

siddhirna jāyate tasya kalpakoṭiśatairapi

te sarve siddhimāyānti mātṛkārṇaprabhāvataḥ

tadā mantro mahāvidyā yathoktaphaladā bhavet

mantravarṇāṃstataḥ kuryāt śodhanaḥ tantrasammatam

sahasraṃ prajapenmantrī mantradoṣapraśāntaye

kṣiptvā cādau śriyañcaiva taddūṣaṇavimuktaye

yasya yatra bhavedbhaktaḥ so'pi mantro'sya sidhyati

amṛtatrayasaṃyogādduṣṭamantro'pi sidhyati

sumanohara-mekhalāyonyādiviśiṣṭamityarthaḥ

bhūmeḥ parigrahaṃ kuryādyāvadāyatanaṃ bhavet

dhanyadhānyakarīṃ vaiśyā śūdrā tu ninditā bhavet

śalyādiśodhanaṃ kuryāt pauruṣaṃ vā khanet tataḥ

diśi saṅkalpayenmantrī maṇḍapañca vibhāgataḥ

pañcahastantu ekakuṇḍapakṣe

tadekakuṇḍaṃ gṛhasyottarabhāge praśastam

lakṣahome tu tatkuryāt gṛhamadhye na dūṣaṇam

caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ toraṇādyairalaṃkṛtam

caturasrāṃ tato vedīṃ maṇḍalāya prakalpayet

kṣetraṃ vibhajya madhye'ṃśe pūrvādi parikalpayet

aṣṭāśāsvaṣṭakuṇḍāni ramyākārāṇyanukramāt

ṣaḍasraṃ paṅkajākāramaṣṭāsraṃ tāni nāmataḥ

samantātkuṇḍametat tu caturasraṃ śubhāvaham

nyaset purastādekāṃśaṃ koṇārddhārddha pramāṇataḥ

sūtrayugmaṃ tato dadyāt kuṇḍaṃ yoninibhaṃ bhavet

arddhacandranibhaṃ kuṇḍaṃ ramaṇīyamidaṃ bhavet

jyāsūtraṃ pātayedagre śarāvārddhatayā yataḥ

sūtratrayaṃ budhaḥ kuryāt tryasraṃ kuṇḍamudāhṛtam

bhrāmayet tena mānena vṛttaṃ kuṇḍamudāhṛtam

bhāgaṃ nyasedekamekaṃ mānenānena madhyataḥ

sūtraṣaṭkaṃ tato dadyāt ṣaḍasraṃ kuṇḍamīritam

ekaṃ bhāgaṃ bahirnyasya bhrāmayet tena varttulam

anyathā mānādhikyāpadyeḥ

tadagrāṇi caturthe syurvṛttānyevaṃ prakalpayediti bhaṭṭaḥ

aṣṭāsraṃ kuṇḍametaddhi tantravidbhirudīritam

evaṃ proktāni kuṇḍāni kathyete sruksruvau tataḥ

śrīparṇī-śiṃśapā-kṣīra-śākhiṣvekatamaṃ guruḥ

hastamātraṃ bāhumātram

viṃśatyaṃśairbhaved daṇḍo vedī tairaṣṭābhirbhavet

vedītryaṃśena vistāraḥ kaṇṭhasya parikīrttitaḥ

kaniṣṭhāṃgulimānena sarpiṣo nirgamāya ca

vidadhīta bahistasyā ekāṃśenābhito'vaṭam

aṃśenaikena parito dalāni parikalpayet

daṇḍamūlāgrayoḥ kumbhau guṇavedāṃgulaiḥ kramāt

ṣaḍbhiraṃśaiḥ pṛṣṭhabhāgo vedyāḥ kūrmākṛtirbhavet

mukhasya pṛṣṭhabhāge'syāḥ samproktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ srucaḥ

dvāviṃśatyā daṇḍamānam aṃśairetasya kīrttitam

aṃśadvayena vilikhet paṅke mṛgapadākṛtim

gaṇḍī kaṅkaṇakumbhayoriti bhāguriḥ

vidadhyātvāśvatthapatre saṃkṣipte homakarmaṇita

putrapradaṃ yonikuṇḍamarddhendvābhaṃ śubhapradam

dṛṣṭidaṃ rogaśamanaṃ kuṇḍamaṣṭāsramīritam

māraṇe ca tathā yojyaṃ varttulaṃ mantribhiḥ sadā

uccāṭe cānile kuṇḍaṃ yāmye ca māraṇaṃ bhavet

vaiṇyānāmarddhacandrābhaṃ śūdrāṇāṃ tryasramīritam

sarvādhikārikaṃ kuṇdaṃ sarvadaṃ caturasrakam

mānāṃgulena karttavyaṃ nānyenāpi kadācana

yajamānasya karttavyaṃ nānyasyāpi kadācana

mānakṛdyajamānaḥ syādviduṣāmeva nirṇayaḥ

madhyasva dairghyamānena mānāṃgulamudāhṛtam

pramāṇāṃguṣṭhametat tu sarvakarmaṇi cāpare

adīrghayojitairhastaścaturviṃśatibhirbhavet

kanyasaṃ ṣaḍbhiruddiṣṭamaṃgulaṃ munisattamaiḥ

dvihastamayute tacca lakṣahome catuṣkaram

daśahastāt paraṃ kuṇḍaṃ nāsti home mahītale

lakṣāṇāṃ daśakaṃ yāvat tāvaddhastena varddhayet

sarvakuṇḍeṣu sarvatra varddhayed vidhināmunā

pūcche tu dukhasambhūtiḥ kroḍe sarvārthasādhanam

siddhāḥ

nāgaḥ

khātādhike bhavedrogī khātahīne dhanakṣayaḥ

mekhalārahite śoko hyadhike vittasaṃkṣayaḥ

apatyadhvaṃsanaṃ proktaṃ kuṇḍaṃ kaṇṭhavivarjitam

tṛtīyā tāmasī proktā mekhalānāṃ vinirṇayaḥ

utsedhāyāmato jñeyātvekārddhāṃguli sammitā

hastamātramite kuṇḍe vedāgninayanāṃgulāḥ

caturhaste tu tā jñeyā vasutarkayugāṃgulāḥ

vistārotsedhato jñeyā mekhalāḥ sarvato budhaiḥ

mekhalānāṃ bhavedantaḥ parito nemiraṃgulā

nābhiyonyorekamānasya dṛṣṭatvāt

kuryāt gajoṣṭhavattāntu kuṇḍavitsarvalakṣaṇam

ṣaṭ-caturdvyaṃgulāyāmavistāronnatiśālinī

yavadvayapramāṇena yonyagramapi varddhayet

bāhyasthamekhalāvāhyasthalādārabhya kārayet

sarandhrakamityubhayatra saṃvadhyate

madhyato madhyaparyantamityarthaḥ

randhraṃ kuryāt tathā vidvān dvitīyamekhalopari

bahiraṃśadvayenāṣṭau patrāṇi parikalpayet

namo'nta iti paurāṇikamantraparam

na tu oṃ ādityāya namaḥ, etat pādyaṃ sūryāya namaḥ ityādi

tenasvāhāntamantre svāhāntaraṃ na vaktavyam

pañcāṃgulaṃ bahistyaktvā dhārayet śaṅkhamudrayeti

anukte tu havirdravye tilājyaṃ havirucyate

vibhūtiñcāgnikāryeṇa sarvasiddhiñca vindati

balivaiśyādikañcaiva brāhmaṇaḥ samupācaret

hutvā stutvā namaskṛtya visṛjedindumaṇḍale

pūrvādidikkrameṇaiva tato homaṃ samācaret

kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile vāpi vīkṣaṇādibhiḥ saṃskṛte

tenaiva prokṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ varmaṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ matam

udagagreṣu brahmavaivasvatendūnpūjayet

evaṃ krameṇa pūjayet

tato homaṃ prakūrvīta devatāsannidhānataḥ

puṣpāñjalīn dadyāt, sundarī pakṣe tu bālayā

tato raṃ vahnyāsanāya namaḥ

sundarī pakṣe tu kāmeśvaraṃ kāmeśvarīṃ pūjayet

śrotriyāṇāṃ gehajañca vanasthaṃ vāthavā haret

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena vahnimuktaṃ samāharet

huṃ-phaḍantena mūlena kravyādāṃśaṃ parityajet

ātmano'bhimukhaṃ devyā yonāvenaṃ kṣipet

suvarṇavarṇamamalaṃ samiddhaṃ viśvatomukham ityupatiṣṭhet

vajrādyastrebhyo namaḥ

svāheti agnimukhe

tataḥ saṃkalpaṃ vidhāya tattatkalpoktadravyeṇa homaṃ kuryāt

iti saṃkṣepahomavidhiḥ

tato homaṃ prakurvīta devatāsannidhānataḥ

tato mūlamuccārya oṃ kuṇḍāya namaḥ iti kuṇḍaṃ sampūjayet

huṃphaḍantena mūlena kravyādāṃśaṃ parityajet

mantreṇa vahniṃ jvālayet

ityanena vahnimupatiṣṭhet

etāḥ sāttvikayāgakarmaṇi

nyāsantu bījayogena pūrvavat karmabhedena sarvatra boddhavyaḥ

vahnimantrāḥ samuddiṣṭā jihvānāṃ saptadeśikaiḥ

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣu sahasrārciṣe hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi

sarvatra ādau praṇavaḥ

tārādyagnipadādyāḥ syurnatyantā vahnimūrttayaḥ

jaṭābhiḥ

brahmādidevatāḥ pūjyāḥ

krūre sarayūṃ viśvamūrtaye namaḥ ityādi

bahurūpā yathākhyātā dakṣiṇottara saṃsthitā

svasvadikṣu pūjayet

sthāpayet

taddarbhayugmaṃ agnau visarjayet
